“It is not easy, is it?”

Jeokyeon rubbed his aching eye and nodded. Truly, Geom Mugeuk was both the easiest leader to protect and, at the same time, the most difficult.

Looking up at the sky where Geom Mugeuk had disappeared, Jeokyeon said quietly,

“But it will not be easy for his enemies either.”

Chapter 270: Can You Handle It?
-------------------------------
“Are you not going back?”

Despite the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s words, Gowol merely alternated his gaze between the Go board and the booklet in his hand.

“All the work is finished now, isn’t it? Let’s go back!”

They had finally completed the task of establishing an intelligence organization in the Central Plains.

The name Geommugeuk had given the organization was Eunwol. Gowol had not appended Hall or Order to it. He had decided to use the name exactly as Geommugeuk had given it.

“Let’s go!”

“I’m not going.”

“Why?”

“I’ll return when I can win at Go against the Chief Strategist.”

Of course, the Pungcheon Cult Leader understood the underlying intention in Gowol’s words. It meant he would not return until he had managed Eunwol into a better organization than the Tongcheongak led by the Chief Strategist. No matter how well an organization was built, the difference between one managed directly by its leader and one that wasn’t was bound to manifest. Especially right after its creation, a time when the leader’s diligence was most needed.

“Do you think the Second Young Master will even appreciate this?”

“He’s not the Second Young Master. He’s the Young Cult Leader.”

“Same man, whatever you call him. Now that he’s achieved his goal, you’ll become leftover rice, you and the demonic venerables. He’ll shove you in some corner, and when you meet, he’ll say something like, ‘Who are you again?’ He won’t even call you ‘that strategist’ anymore. He’ll probably say, ‘Don’t get in my way, move aside. The Young Cult Leader is coming through.’ That’s just life, whether you like it or not. Who wants to look after others forever?”

Gowol spoke while still looking at the Go board and the Go book in his hand.

“Aren’t you tired of saying such things by now?”

“I’ll say the same thing over and over until I die. And you listen over and over. That’s what close friends do.”

“Then aren’t we too close?”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader said nothing more and walked to the window.

“That won’t work! Pretending to be sulky.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader, who had been walking toward the window, naturally turned around as if he had not meant to go there, and this time walked toward the bed. Just as he was pretending to have a fever and touching his forehead, Gowol beat him to it.

“Stop pretending to be ill!”

At that, the Pungcheon Cult Leader eventually returned to his original spot.

“Let’s just curse the Second Young Master. That’s the only thing that actually relieves my frustration. Where did we leave off?”

“‘Move aside, the Young Cult Leader is coming through.’”

Despite saying he was tired of it, Gowol had listened properly to everything.

“Now that he’s become the Young Cult Leader and Eunwol is complete, you’ll be pushed to the back. Before long, he’ll casually ask about merging Eunwol with Tongcheongak. What will you do then?”

“If he says to merge, I’ll merge.”

“No wonder the Second Young Master looks at you like such a tasty morsel. He’ll tear off all your flesh and lick every bone clean.”

Then, a familiar voice was heard from behind.

“You truly slander me every time we meet.”

Surprised, they turned to see Geommugeuk standing there with a smile.

“You truly have a remarkable talent for showing up only when I’m speaking ill of you.”

“Isn’t most of your day spent speaking ill of me?”

“Sometimes I speak ill of you all day, even in my sleep.”

He spoke thus, but the Pungcheon Cult Leader’s face was full of delight.

To the Pungcheon Cult Leader, Geommugeuk respectfully cupped his hands in greeting.

“You’ve worked hard, Cult Leader.”

“Why so sudden? Is this the divine art of giving rice cakes to someone you hate?”

“I heard that Eunwol has been completed. If the Cult Leader had not helped from the side, things could not have proceeded so smoothly.”

“So you two have been in contact again behind my back. Excluding me!”

This time, Gowol formally greeted Geommugeuk.

“I am only now formally greeting you. Please accept my congratulations on becoming the Young Cult Leader.”

“You played a large part in it.”

“Even if a broom had been placed in my position, it would not have hindered the overall trend.”

Then the Pungcheon Cult Leader butted in.

“Stop acting so humble. He’ll really think it’s true. Say that you worked yourself half to death. No matter what you say, humans only think about how hard their own lives are.”

Gowol said with a smile.

“Our Cult Leader worked very hard. Please acknowledge and praise him.”

“You’ve worked hard.”

At that, the Pungcheon Cult Leader raised his voice.

“Stop pretending to be nice and speak your true feelings!”

Geommugeuk and Gowol looked at each other and smiled.

Then Geommugeuk’s gaze turned toward the Go board behind them.

“Are you finally becoming a perfect strategist who even plays Go well?”

Geommugeuk walked over to the Go board.

Gowol also stood before the Go board with him.

“Shall we play a match?”

“I have only just learned, so I am still far from good.”

However, contrary to his words, the board position far surpassed the level of a beginner.

“This fight is fierce.”

At Geommugeuk’s words, Gowol uttered something meaningful.

“Does it not resemble this matter?”

The moment he heard that question, Geommugeuk knew. Gowol had already fully grasped what had happened at the Honam Branch. He wondered if Gowol had been playing Go alone while pondering that problem.

“This move was thrown to save stones that would become dead. It is too deep to be called a probe, yet too reckless to be a move to rescue dead stones.”

Gowol’s gaze shifted from the Go board to Geommugeuk.

“It is a move played by the Martial Alliance to save the Cheonhwamun.”

Indeed, Gowol already knew the developing situation. Furthermore, he had grasped things that even Geommugeuk did not know.

“Jin Hagun, the grandson of the Martial Alliance Leader and the commander of the Demon Extermination Legion, exercised his unique authority to move the Martial Alliance. The Cult Leader accepted their request. Of course, I understand it is customary that if we accept their request now, the Martial Alliance will later accept a request from our cult.”

Geommugeuk nodded. Since this was also a test for his son, his father would be watching this move with interest.

“But do they know what Seocheong did?”

“I believe they have grasped it to some extent.”

Except for the principal culprit, Seocheong, and Seokpung, who had exposed the crime, all the other Mushinhoe members had been released. Through them, news of what had happened at the banquet hall would have spread to some extent.

“And yet they push ahead?”

“Because the gains are great enough to endure the criticism.”

Geommugeuk shook his head. The Martial Alliance Leader he had seen had been a domineering yet fair and just man.

“Perhaps their grandson is also being tested.”

Gowol immediately understood what he meant and nodded.

“How should we respond now?”

Geommugeuk had rushed all the way here to hear that answer.

The rush had been worthwhile. Gowol had already established a plan.

“That Cheonhwamun has been able to tightrope between our cult, the Martial Alliance, and the Demonic Alliance until now is partly due to their own strength, but also because of the thirty-odd small and medium sects that follow them. And most of those sects are not forces that follow the righteous path, but ones that follow our cult and the Demonic Alliance.”

“They must be dissatisfied that Cheonhwamun has joined hands with the righteous faction.”

“Exactly. We shall absorb all their sects in this opportunity. If that happens, the Martial Alliance will end up making a fuss without gaining any substance.”

“An excellent plan!”

Geommugeuk was greatly pleased.

But Gowol was also aware of the problem in his plan.

“The problem is the reaction of the Martial Alliance and Cheonhwamun if we do so. They will certainly not stand still, so we must prepare for that.”

It was a valid point. They could not simply watch such a situation unfold.

After pondering briefly, Geommugeuk picked up a white stone.

“If we try to save dead stones by force, we must be punished. However….”

Clack!

Geommugeuk placed the stone on a spot away from where the fight was unfolding.

“If we rush to capture them openly, they will struggle desperately to survive, so I intend to make a move from the opposite side of where I am trying to capture.”

“Strike the east to attack the west.”

At Gowol’s words, Geommugeuk smiled meaningfully.

“And in truth, that person is to our east.”

* * *

Geommugeuk dashed toward the east.

His swift movement technique, having achieved the Great Perfection of the Ten Stars, shrank the Central Plains before him.

In that place he reached faster than a bird, Geommugeuk met him. The welcome person he had sought was dining at an inn as always today.

“—I told you to eat a balanced diet, yet you are still picky.”

At the flying sound transmission, Bisain smirked.

Illang, who was standing nearby, spoke to him.

“Why are you smiling?”

“I simply thought of a tactless person.”

After Yawolhan died, Bisain had completely seized real power. He had become the solid successor to the Demonic Alliance in name and truth, and many followed him. It would not be an overstatement to say all those results were thanks to Geommugeuk.

“—Let us meet at the cliff tonight.”

“—Enough with the meetings already.”

* * *

That night, when Bisain arrived at the cliff, Geommugeuk was sitting astride the edge of the cliff.

“I don’t know why, but every time I see your back, I want to push you off.”

“Then push me off firmly today. Once you realize it’s useless, you won’t feel that way next time.”

Bisain came and stood beside Geommugeuk.

“Why have you come again?”

“Why else? I came because I need your help.”

“What kind of demonic cultist asks for help from the Demonic Alliance he will one day face?”

He spoke thus, but Bisain’s heart was pounding. Just when his repeating daily life felt tedious, Geommugeuk would abruptly appear before him.

Remaining seated, Geommugeuk looked up at Bisain’s face and spoke.

“Oh, from this angle, your face looks quite handsome. From here, your scar looks cool, too. If you have a woman you admire, make sure to seat her here and stand there.”

“Stop talking nonsense and tell me why you came.”

“Let’s do one thing together.”

“What do you want to do?”

“Recently, the Martial Alliance has been acting greedy. But if it’s greed, we are not the sort to be left out, are we?”

Bisain’s expression turned slightly serious.

“Are you speaking of the Cheonhwamun matter?”

As expected, Bisain already knew of the matters regarding the Cheonhwamun. He had heard that the Cheonhwamun’s successor had been captured by Geommugeuk. He had naturally expected them to ask for help, but unexpectedly, they had joined hands with the Martial Alliance. Because of that, the Demonic Alliance and Bisain had been on high alert.

“As expected, you knew everything.”

“With you making such a fuss, how could I not know? Even the children playing in the streets probably know.”

“As you know, I love being the center of attention.”

“So? Why did you come?”

Separate from his goodwill toward Geommugeuk, this matter was extremely significant. A great neutral force was taking one side.

“We cannot simply stand by and watch, can we?”

“Someone like you could resolve this alone, could you not?”

He knew better than anyone how outstanding Geommugeuk was. That was why Bisain was suspicious of him.

“I could have resolved it alone, of course. If it were not for my father.”

“What do you mean?”

“An official order was issued. To release the Cheonhwamun successor. In other words, it means not to clash head-on with them.”

“And so? You intend to use me in your stead?”

“Something like that.”

Bisain spoke firmly.

“I refuse.”

To him as he tried to turn away, Geommugeuk said.

“Let us divide it into three.”

In that moment, Bisain stopped. How could he simply leave after hearing those words?

“What do you mean?”

“Most of the small and medium sects following the Cheonhwamun are ones that follow our cult and the Demonic Alliance. They will have much dissatisfaction with the Cheonhwamun’s unilateral decision this time. Let us absorb them all.”

Bisain was startled. He had been so focused on the Cheonhwamun that he had not thought that far.

“Why do you give me an opportunity as well, instead of absorbing them all yourself?”

Geommugeuk answered honestly.

“It is for three reasons. First, as I said earlier, it is difficult for me to act unilaterally and clash with them. But if you intervene, the situation changes.”

“The second reason?”

“What would I do by forcibly dragging those who wish to go under you? They would only betray me later. If we were in a desperate situation, I would not be choosy, but I do not wish to recklessly expand my forces merely for show. That is the old way.”

Bisain’s scar twitched. He pondered whether Geommugeuk had some hidden motive, but he could not tell. Had it been someone else, he might have grasped the intention more easily; but because it was Geommugeuk, it was truly difficult.

“What is the final reason?”

“Because I’m curious. As you know, the Cheonhwa Sect has mainly dealt with our cult and with you people. Yet when a crisis struck, they joined hands with the Murim Alliance. Under normal circumstances, shouldn’t they have joined hands with you? With their lives and fate on the line, they acted against all common sense? What hidden circumstances could there be? Who was the one who led that decision? Aren’t you curious?”

Bi Sa-in answered honestly as well.

“I am curious. No, I’m even more curious.”

“In truth, I think that reason alone is enough for you to step forward.”

After pondering for a moment, Bi Sa-in suddenly said,

“What if I get greedy in the process? What if I try to absorb the Cheonhwa Sect as well?”

Since the Cheonhwa Sect had maintained as much exchange with the Sado Alliance as with the Demon Cult, it was not impossible for them to join hands with the Sado Alliance.

“Then be greedy.”

“What?”

“If you can, join hands with the Cheonhwa Sect.”

“Do you hate those orthodox bastards that much?”

“That is not why. It’s because I intend to absorb the Cheonhwa Sect too. Since that is what I want as well, I can’t refuse to acknowledge what you want. Let’s compete. To see who will have the Cheonhwa Sect.”

Bi Sa-in looked down at Geom Mugeuk in silence. From the moment they had first met until now, he had made him feel as though there was a wall before him. But it was not a wall that pressed down on him. It was a wall he wanted to climb over. A wall that spurred him on, telling him not to remain as he was.

Bi Sa-in suddenly said,

“I’ve started wanting to shove you over again.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk held out his hand, asking to be helped up.

With an expression of disbelief, Bi Sa-in shook his head.

“I really think you need to seek counsel from the Divine Physician.”

“Our cult’s Demon Physician is so smitten with me that it’s impossible. If you won’t give me a hand, I’ll have to get up on my own. Oof. My back.”

Geom Mugeuk made a show of groaning as he rose from his seat.

Bi Sa-in made his final decision.

“I’ll see you in Hunan.”

It meant he would accept the proposal.

Geom Mugeuk said to him,

“Bring masters who can create a menacing atmosphere. We don’t know what might pop out from their side. And I need a pretext to move as well.”

At that, Bi Sa-in said provocatively,

“Can you handle it?”

“Why would I handle it? I’ll call people too. Demon Venerables even more frightening than you lot.”

“You truly never lose, not even once.”

Bi Sa-in turned and vanished into the darkness.

“You grow more impressive by the day!”

At the words that came from behind him, Bi Sa-in ended up letting out a wry laugh despite himself.

When he turned back to say something, Geom Mugeuk was already gone from that spot. He was flying far away, among the stars in the night sky.

Looking up at Geom Mugeuk and those densely scattered stars, Bi Sa-in said quietly,

“I will handle you. I will make sure I become that kind of person.”

Chapter 271: We Came Too.
-------------------------
Seo Baekjung, master of the Cheonhwa Sect, was looking out the window.

It was the same scene as always: martial artists laughing as they crossed the training ground.

A long while had passed since his son had been released, yet the Demon Cult remained quiet.

‘As expected! Truly remarkable.’

Seo Baekjung thought of the man in the bamboo hat. He had asked because he believed the man could do it, but he had not imagined it would be handled so cleanly.

The days of walking the tightrope between the righteous, the evil, and the demonic were now over. Since he owed the righteous faction a debt, the time had come to join hands with them and move forward. Of course, he would have to join hands in a way that benefited the Cheonhwa Sect as much as possible.

Just then, his trusted subordinate Yu Maeng entered.

“You called for me?”

Seo Baekjung gave him a secret order.

“Send this invitation to the leader of the Demon Extermination Unit, and inform the factions that follow us as well.”

He intended to hold a secret banquet.

He knew that the leaders of the sects who followed him were dissatisfied with his decision to join hands with the righteous faction. He had to soothe their discontent as quickly as possible. Gathering them had taken ages, but collapse could happen in an instant.

This was not something he could accomplish through persuasion alone. Jin Hagun, leader of the Demon Extermination Unit and the future leader of the Murim Alliance, had to come in person and placate them.

‘Now then, I will gather the people, so show me what you are capable of.’

* * *

Jong Yeom (宗染), leader of the Small Sword Gang (小劍幫), was more nervous than he had ever been.

As the head of a faction that ranked within the top ten in Hunan, he had never been this tense before. He paced back and forth across the main hall, drank water, drank tea, sat down, stood up, went in and out of the privy, strolled through the courtyard, and returned inside—repeating it all several times.

His son, Jong Tae (宗泰), found his father’s behavior utterly unfamiliar. His father, who had always appeared imposing and dignified, was this nervous. He was even sweating coldly.

“Who on earth is the guest coming today?”

His father had sent all the gang members outside on one pretext or another. At present, only a small number of trustworthy martial artists remained within the gang compound. It was obvious that he intended to receive the guest in secret.

Someone important was certainly coming, but his father would not tell him who.

“There is nothing good in knowing ahead of time. Even if you learn their identities later, you must not be shaken under any circumstances. Just quietly follow my instructions.”

At that moment, a subordinate reported from outside.

“The guests have arrived.”

The door opened, and a man entered the main hall.

The instant Jong Tae saw him, he froze. The young man’s face bore a terrifying scar. Not only that, he was faintly emitting an aura of evil.

‘An expert of the evil faction!’

The one who entered was none other than Bi Sain, heir to the Sado Alliance. Thirteen martial artists followed behind him. They were the Sado Thirteen Wolves, who guarded Bi Sain.

They spread out to the four corners of the main hall and took control of the space. The presence of each and every one of them was formidable.

They alone were enough to make Jong Tae feel suffocated, but the truly frightening people entered last.

The two who came in at the end were exuding truly dreadful auras, so it was only natural that Jong Tae’s knees trembled. They were absolute masters belonging to the Seven Great Experts of the Evil Path (邪道七大高手).

Goeak (怪惡).

Having cultivated Chamgyeok Cheorin (斬擊鐵人), a type of bodily martial art that used the entire body in combat, to its extreme, he was said to be capable of crumpling an iron sword with his bare hands as though it were paper.

He was infamous throughout murim for his eccentric and cruel nature, and was known for killing those who got on his nerves only after subjecting them to excruciating pain. He was an evil faction master no one wished to meet even in a dream.

Gwangseom (光閃).

He was known as the foremost master of swift swordsmanship in the evil faction. The Churoe Samgeom (追雷三劍) he had mastered was said to be the fastest among the swift swords of the Evil Path.

He was taciturn by nature, making it impossible to know what thoughts he lived with. His sword was fast, but his mouth was slow; he too was an expert ranked among the top seven of the evil faction.

Bi Sain had accepted Geom Mugeuk’s advice and truly brought two terrifying experts with him.

“I greet the Young Supreme. I am Jong Yeom, who leads the Small Sword Gang.”

“A pleasure. I have long heard of Gang Leader Jong’s reputation.”

After exchanging greetings, Bi Sain introduced Goeak and Gwangseom.

Upon learning their identities, Jong Yeom and Jong Tae turned pale and bowed deeply. The absolute masters of the evil faction, whom they had only heard of in rumors, were right before their eyes.

Only then did Jong Tae understand why his father had been so nervous. He also understood why his father had not told him who the other party was. If he had known in advance, he would have been trembling beside his father.

“This is my son.”

Jong Tae cupped his fist respectfully and greeted them. It was a difficult and dangerous occasion, but Jong Yeom had deliberately brought his son to sit in on it. He knew well just how great an experience a meeting with such great figures would be for a martial artist’s life.

“Your son is tall and handsome, and looks very intelligent.”

“He is still lacking in many ways. Go and brew some tea.”

Jong Tae brought the tea, tense from head to toe.

The teacups trembled in his hands as he carried them. Bi Sain saw the trembling, and Jong Yeom saw it too, but neither said a word. After all, it would have been stranger if he did not tremble while serving tea to the heir of the Sado Alliance, to Goeak, and to Gwangseom.

After bringing the tea, he stood a short distance away and listened to the conversation between his father and Bi Sain.

“Please take the seat of honor.”

Jong Yeom offered him the seat of honor, but Bi Sain did not sit there.

“The seat of honor belongs to Gang Leader Jong.”

It was as though Bi Sain was saying, I am not someone who has come to take your place.

Once Jong Yeom carefully sat in his own seat, Bi Sain immediately got to the point.

“You must know that the Cheonhwa Sect has joined hands with the Murim Alliance.”

“Yes, I know.”

“I will ask directly. Does Gang Leader Jong wish to follow the righteous faction?”

Before receiving an answer, Bi Sain quickly added,

“Please do not misunderstand and think I brought these two peerless experts here to pressure you. I brought them to show you that from now on, we are the ones who will protect the Small Sword Gang.”

Perhaps because Bi Sain had warned them beforehand, Goeak and Gwangseom revealed no aura whatsoever and quietly drank their tea.

Because they remained still, Bi Sain seemed even more impressive. They were men far better suited to terrifying threats, yet he was someone who could have them quietly drink tea.

“No matter what answer you give, our alliance will not harm the Small Sword Gang in any way. I promise on my name. So speak freely.”

“Yes, I will.”

Jong Yeom knew that the fate of the Small Sword Gang would change depending on his answer.

“Our gang does not wish to follow the righteous faction. I am a man who breaks out in hives at the very thought of the righteous faction. The reason we have followed the Cheonhwa Sect until now is because they maintained deep ties with the Sado Alliance.”

Before making his decision, Jong Yeom calmly expressed his concerns.

“Hunan has been a region where the Demon Cult’s power has been strong for generations. Our gang remained under the shadow of the Cheonhwa Sect in order to avoid the Demon Cult’s blade.”

At that, Bi Sain said firmly, his eyes filled with trust.

“We will block that blade from now on.”

Jong Yeom’s worries continued.

“The Cheonhwa Sect will not look kindly upon us either.”

Bi Sain answered even more firmly.

“Then the Sado Alliance will not look kindly upon the Cheonhwa Sect either.”

Jong Yeom gazed at Bi Sain’s face. At that sight, his son Jong Tae was inwardly surprised and impressed. To think that his father, who had been trembling so badly, could show such a dignified side. Without being intimidated in the least, he was securing the promises that had to be secured.

“Can you promise me that?”

“I promise in the name of the young alliance lord of the Sado Alliance.”

Jong Yeom did not drag things out any longer. He immediately rose from his seat, cupped his fist respectfully, and said,

“From now on, the Small Sword Gang will share its fate with the Sado Alliance.”

Bi Sain rose from his seat and cupped his fist.

“The Sado Alliance will share its fate with the Small Sword Gang.”

It was the moment the Small Sword Gang’s new fate was decided.

Bi Sain immediately took his leave. There were still many sects he had to visit.

Before departing, Bi Sain said to Jong Yeom,

“Many sects will share the same will as the Small Sword Gang. So you may proceed proudly.”

And so Bi Sain, the Sado Thirteen Wolves, Goeak, and Gwangseom left the place.

Only after they had gone did Jong Tae let out a sigh of relief.

“Were you trying to watch your son die of fright?”

“As you live in murim from now on, you will experience such things many times. When that happens, you must deal with them calmly, just as you did today.”

He knew his son would handle things well. If his son had been frivolous and foolish, he would not have had him sit in on today’s meeting in the first place.

“But shouldn’t we have received their promise in writing, perhaps with a contract?”

“If they choose to break their promise, what meaning would a mere contract have?”

Jong Tae was seeing a new side of his father. Usually, he bowed and scraped before the Cheonhwa Sect Master, always laughing heartily. Jong Tae had thought that was the kind of father he had.

“To make such a decision at once… I am truly astonished.”

“I did not make it at once.”

“What?”

“From the day they secretly sent word asking to meet until now, I have not slept a wink.”

Jong Yeom looked at his son and said,

“It was a matter tied to all our lives. How could I simply make a decision?”

Having newly confirmed his father’s outstanding qualities, Jong Tae bowed his head respectfully. Seeing how moved he was in this moment, it seemed he had not truly respected his father until now.

“Thank you for allowing me to be here.”

And even in this moving moment, the one concern above all else was still the Demon Cult.

“Will the Demon Cult really stay still?”

“Of course they won’t. Now that the Sado Alliance has moved, they too will begin to move.”

Jong Yeom’s gaze turned to the door through which Bi Sain had walked out.

“With the young cult leader of the Demon Cult coming to the Central Plains, the sleeping murim has begun to stir like a dragon.”

“No matter what comes, I will respond flexibly like bamboo.”

It was something his father had always said. But now his father’s thinking had changed. Just as the heir of the Sado Alliance had come to this place today, the Small Sword Gang of the future would have to be led by his son.

Jong Yeom hoped his son would live his own life safely rather than become a hero of murim. Just as he himself had lived.

“Your father survived by conducting himself like bamboo. But in a great wind, even bamboo will eventually snap. You must live like flowing water.”

This was what he wished to tell his son before the tide of change.

* * *

The gatekeeper of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s Hunan branch was guarding the branch’s main gate today as always.

When one stood watch all day like this, the hardest thing to endure was the boredom. And so he would exchange all sorts of idle talk with those on duty with him. Today as well, a subordinate standing watch beside him relayed a rumor.

“It seems the Cheonhwa Sect is planning to hold a secret banquet.”

“A banquet? Where did you hear that?”

“I know someone who supplies food for the Cheonhwa Sect’s banquets. There’s also a rumor that some incredible experts from the Murim Alliance will be coming.”

That was not the only rumor.

“There are also rumors that experts from the Sado Alliance have entered Hunan. The way things are moving feels far from ordinary.”

Both rumors were displeasing from the Demon Cult’s perspective.

The gatekeeper said to his subordinate,

“The atmosphere has been bad lately, so don’t run your mouth for no reason.”

“But are we going to back down to those righteous faction bastards just like this?”

“Enough!”

“Yes.”

After a brief pause, the gatekeeper said,

“As I see it, the young cult leader is not someone who will simply back down.”

All of his subordinates nodded. After all, they had personally experienced the process of him dealing with the Hunan branch leader and capturing the Cheonhwa Sect’s heir. The young cult leader was clearly someone who defied their expectations.

At that moment, one of the martial artists shouted,

“Someone is coming.”

Far in the distance, two people were walking toward them.

They were merely walking slowly, yet the feeling was chilling. The gatekeeper wondered if he was the only one who felt that way and looked at his subordinates, but they too wore faces taut with tension. One subordinate had already gripped the hilt of his sword tightly.

The gatekeeper’s gaze turned back to them.

They were only walking slowly, so why was he trembling like this?

Gradually, their figures became clearer.

One of his subordinates spoke in a trembling voice.

“Should we raise the alarm?”

Normally, it would have been nonsense. Someone was visiting the branch, and without even knowing who they were, they would raise the alarm? A demon practitioner getting frightened? This was truly something that warranted beating the subordinate who had suggested it with a club.

And yet the gatekeeper hesitated. Should he raise the alarm?

In the meantime, the two drew even closer. The closer they came, the greater his fear grew. Now every one of his subordinates had their hands on their swords. Martial artists who gave off such a presence existed in this world? If they were enemies, he had a premonition that all of them would die.

“Raise the alarm!”

One demon practitioner ran inside, and soon the sound of a bell rang out.

Clang! Claaang!

Even as the alarm bell resounded, the two continued walking toward them at the same pace.

And so the two came all the way to the main gate.

The thin old man with cold eyes carried a massive greatsaber on his back. The other man was a muscular giant with a face so fearsome it was difficult to meet his eyes.

When the gatekeeper sensed that the aura emanating from the two of them was demonic energy, only then did he feel relieved. Even so, his heart pounded madly, and his body trembled. Faced with a presence so overwhelming it seemed to choke him, the gatekeeper finally understood.

‘They’re Demon Supremes!’

Only then did the demon practitioners guarding the main gate realize who the two were. Though they had never seen them in person, had they not heard countless tales of these Demon Supremes? That enormous greatsaber, those enormous fists, and that powerful demonic energy and presence no one could imitate.

They were none other than the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the Fist Demon.

The martial artists at the main gate all bowed at once to show their respect. Rejoicing, they shouted inwardly.

‘Our side has come too!’

With his small, fierce eyes, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon asked in a low voice,

“Where is the young cult leader?”

Chapter 272: Why Us of All People?
----------------------------------
There was someone blocking Jin Hageon, who was walking toward the carriage. It was his younger sister, Jin Haryeong.

“You're attending the banquet?”

“What brings you here?”

“What else? I came to see off my dear brother.”

“Why are you doing something you never do?”

Having lost their parents early, the two had grown up relying on each other. Thus, they cared for each other deeply. However, it was the first time Jin Haryeong had come out to see him off like this.

“Is it because of that Geom Mugeuk fellow?”

“What do you mean?”

“Did you come out because you're worried about him?”

Jin Hageon knew that his sister had formed a connection with Geom Mugeuk during the Soryongjeon last time. He also knew that thanks to Geom Mugeuk’s help, his sister had emerged unscathed then.

Jin Haryeong could neither deny it nor admit it.

If she said she was worried about her brother, it would hurt his pride; if she said she was worried about Geom Mugeuk, it would invite needless misunderstanding.

“He is not an easy opponent. Don't ever let your guard down. That's what I came to tell you.”

“I am aware of his extraordinariness as well. I shall be careful.”

Jin Haryeong grabbed her brother's arm as he tried to board the carriage.

“You must approach him without prejudice. Don't think, 'He's a demonic practitioner, so he must be like this.' He is not the kind of person you think he is.”

Jin Hageon could not tell exactly what his sister was worried about. After all, he had never met Geom Mugeuk even once.

“That Geom Mugeuk has grown too large. If we cannot break his momentum now, we may not be able to stop him later.”

Jin Haryeong had also been hearing news of Geom Mugeuk. The news was astonishing. She thought back to when he had first introduced himself with an alias.

“I am Geomyeon. Not the Yeon of fate or connection, but the Yeon of smoke. I will remain like this for a moment, then vanish like smoke.”

Perhaps to her, he was not Geom Mugeuk, but Geomyeon. Because when everything was over, he had truly disappeared like smoke. That must be why she thought of him from time to time. She heard he had become the Young Cult Leader. She wanted to say at least a word of congratulations.

Unaware of such feelings, Jin Hageon regarded Geom Mugeuk as an enemy.

“I believe that righteousness can overcome any evil. So do not worry.”

Then Jin Haryeong spoke cautiously.

“What if he is a Demonic Hero?”

A martial artist of the demonic path who nonetheless upholds righteousness. She hadn’t realized it when she saw Geom Mugeuk, but whenever she thought of him after parting, the word “Demonic Hero” came to mind. One who is not bound by dogma or formalities yet steadfastly protects what must be protected—thus, not the stifling kind of righteous hero who fails because of this or hesitates because of that, but the kind of Demonic Hero who unflinchingly smashes everything apart.

“Could such a person exist? Such things are merely fabrications by idle gossips. Upholding righteousness is not such an easy feat. It is something demonic practitioners obsessed with the strong devouring the weak can never accomplish.”

With that, the carriage carrying Jin Hageon departed. The martial artists escorting him followed on horseback.

Watching the sight, Jin Haryeong let out a light sigh.

“I was telling you to let go of such prejudices.”

She clearly had faith in both her brother and Geom Mugeuk.

But today, Jin Hageon was moving to contend for hegemony over Murim. When ambitions collided and desires ran rampant, no one could tell what would happen.

Thus, her eyes watching the carriage ride off into the distance were filled with worry. Seeing herself worrying as she never had before, she realized she had become an adult before she knew it, and Murim was hurtling toward a new era.

* * *

“Why us of all people?”

As soon as they met, Hyeolcheon Doma demanded as if picking a fight.

But I could feel it. Doma was excited. Because this was the first time he had been called upon when the help of Demonic Venerables was needed.

“We need to create an atmosphere of terror. As if a state of emergency has been declared in the Honam region even though you've done nothing.”

Then Hyeolcheon Doma glanced at Gwonma and nodded with an expression that said, 'If so, you chose well.' There were few people in the world who could show such a reaction in front of Gwonma.

“Then why am I here?”

Hyeolcheon Doma had naturally assumed that the atmosphere of terror was Gwonma's role.

“Elder, you are in charge of terror.”

“What?”

The surprised Hyeolcheon Doma turned back to Gwonma.

“Then why did you call him?”

“Because I need smart people too.”

“Why, you—!”

Before Hyeolcheon Doma could explode in anger, I ran forward to embrace him.

“Elder! I've missed you.”

In truth, Gwonma was the face, and Hyeolcheon Doma was the fiery one.

Hyeolcheon Doma dodged my attempt to hug him using footwork.

“I've been utterly humiliated here in Honam! You have no idea how much I wished for you two to come!”

“Serves you right. Who were you tormenting?”

“It's a misunderstanding!”

After a chaotic reunion with Hyeolcheon Doma, I greeted Gwonma.

“Master, I've longed to see you.”

Gwonma spoke curtly.

“What help do you need?”

He was the type who, if I said, 'I want to conquer Murim!', would simply reply 'Let's go!' without another word. That was how dependable he was. The joy I felt within that curtness made me all the happier.

It is so good to see you again, Master.

With Hyeolcheon Doma and Gwonma here, it truly felt as if I had gained an army of thousands.

“Now that you two are here, shall we take on the whole of Murim?”

I explained the current situation to both of them.

“...Since the Sado Maeng has entered the fray, we have ample justification to move.”

“You've already decided which sects each of you will absorb with the Sado Maeng.”

As expected, the shrewd Hyeolcheon Doma had grasped the core of this collaboration without needing to be told.

“As expected of you, Elder!”

The sects supporting the Sado Maeng and those supporting the main cult were clearly divided. There was no need to clash with Bisa-in over this matter.

“What of the Sado Maeng?”

“They are currently absorbing sects.”

Then Gwonma stood up abruptly and said.

“Let's go too. No need to waste time.”

Simply showing Gwonma's face would mean this matter was already settled.

* * *

A banquet was prepared at the Cheonhwamun.

The banquet was held at night, and trustworthy martial artists were stationed throughout.

Seocheong was excited at the thought of meeting the man who would become the Murim Alliance Leader.

'They say the successor is young, so I must win him over well. It wouldn't be bad to advance into the Murim Alliance through this opportunity.'

Hadn't he had experience mingling with and leading the young rising stars of Honam? He was confident he could handle youngsters.

On the other hand, Seobaekjung was staring anxiously at the entrance.

The appointed time had passed, yet none of the faction leaders following Cheonhwamun had arrived. It would be disastrous if Jin Hageon arrived before them.

The anxious Seobaekjung pressed his confidant, Yumaeng.

“Why has no one arrived?”

How would Yumaeng know the reason?

When he hesitated, unable to answer, Seobaekjung interrogated him.

“Did you deliver the message properly?”

“Yes.”

“Could you have gotten the date wrong?”

“No. How could I have been negligent with something this important?”

“Then why aren't they coming? Go and check immediately.”

“Yes.”

Even after sending Yumaeng, Seobaekjung could not sit still. He had a bad feeling. They should have arrived early just to read his mood, if nothing else.

Then a subordinate ran in and reported.

“The Myeolmadae Leader has arrived.”

Seobaekjung was startled. Jin Hageon had arrived earlier than the appointed time.

The doors opened, and Myeolmadae martial artists poured in, seizing control of the surroundings.

Watching this, Seocheong was shocked. When the first Myeolmadae warrior entered, he had thought that man was the Myeolmadae Leader. The momentum of the Myeolmadae martial artists was so extraordinary that such a misunderstanding was understandable.

When his eyes met those of a nearby Myeolmadae warrior, Seocheong was the first to avert his gaze. Their aura was incomparable to that of the rising stars he usually mingled with.

Soon after, Jin Hageon entered. The aura of the Leader was different from that of his subordinates. He possessed a certain dignity that made it impossible to treat him lightly.

Seocheong's original plan had been to approach slyly with a “We young martial artists should get along,” and eventually “Let me guide you to a fine place,” but he couldn't even meet Jin Hageon's eyes and simply bowed his head.

An old man entered behind Jin Hageon.

Dressed in pure white robes, holding a sword against his chest as if cradling it, his bearing was as if an immortal had descended.

His presence was truly overwhelming, and Seobaekjung recognized him at a single glance.

'A Sword Immortal!'

He had seen him once from afar in the past. Surrounded by countless experts and receiving grand hospitality, he was a peerless master with an aura one could never forget after a single glance.

Sword Immortal I Haksin.

An absolute master of the orthodox faction and one of Murim's foremost swordsmanship experts, he was also a friend of Murim Alliance Leader Jin Pacheon. Jin Pacheon had asked him to assist his grandson on this journey to Honam.

Seobaekjung rushed out to welcome Jin Hageon.

“Welcome, My Lord.”

“It is a pleasure, Sect Leader.”

“Thanks to your help this time, my son was able to return safely. I will not forget this grace.”

Jin Hageon only nodded and said nothing further about it. Instead, he introduced I Haksin to him.

“This time, the Sword Immortal has specially accompanied me.”

Seobaekjung bowed so low his waist nearly touched the ground.

“It is the greatest honor for my family to meet the Sword Immortal.”

“Greetings.”

The Sword Immortal substituted a greeting with that single word. He was not pleased about absorbing the Cheonhwamun. Accepting those who had not walked the path of righteousness thus far for political reasons did not align with his convictions. But he had come to fulfill his friend's request.

“Come, please sit here.”

Jin Hageon seated himself at the seat of honor, while the Sword Immortal stood slightly apart. By rights, he should have taken the seat of honor, but this time he focused solely on protecting Jin Hageon. Since that matter had already been discussed with Jin Hageon, Jin Hageon focused on the purpose of his visit today.

The Myeolmadae martial artists used silver needles to check the banquet's alcohol and food for poison.

“No one else has arrived yet.”

Seobaekjung flinched at Jin Hageon's words. They should have all been here waiting, but the unthinkable had occurred.

Seobaekjung forced himself to remain calm as he poured wine and spoke.

“Out of a greedy wish to spend time alone with you, I called them for later.”

He then changed the subject by flaunting his power.

“More than thirty sects follow our main sect. If we absorb them all, the Murim Alliance may seize the hegemony of Honam.”

It might seem that since his son had been rescued, he should be the more humble party, but the essence of this matter was different. The one still holding the hilt of the knife was him.

The rescue of his son had to be interpreted thus: how badly must the Murim Alliance have needed the Cheonhwamun to intervene while risking conflict with the demonic cult? Moreover, it wasn't just the Cheonhwamun being absorbed. The thirty-odd factions following Cheonhwamun were being thrown in as a bonus.

'I must lead the negotiations advantageously.'

Meanwhile, Jin Hageon was keeping his words sparse. His opponent was a cunning old fox who had been through countless hardships. When dealing with such a person, one had to speak sparingly and only say what was necessary. A seasoned opponent would find weaknesses in an excess of words.

“As you can guess, most of them are followers of the Cheonma Singyo and the Sado Maeng. To absorb them into the Murim Alliance, you must demonstrate formidable power.”

Jin Hageon was determined to win their hearts. If he could not obtain them, then using his own authority would result in only half a success.

“I wish to show my strength, but they aren't coming.”

As Jin Hageon spoke with a smile, Seobaekjung responded with a jest.

“They must be too intimidated to face the Myeolmadae Leader.”

Afterward, Seobaekjung tried to stall with various words, but no matter how long they waited, the others did not appear.

Seobaekjung was beside himself with anxiety, his hands trembling. He was gambling his family on the wager of a lifetime, yet the very people who needed to appear were not coming. Something had happened.

At first, Jin Ha-gun thought Seo Baek-jung was engaging in a battle of nerves. But he soon sensed that something had gone wrong. Then, at last, the doors to the banquet hall opened.

“They’re here!”

Seo Baek-jung cried out with a delighted expression, looking toward the door. Everyone had hoped a crowd would pour in, but through the open doors stepped a single person.

The moment all eyes focused on him, the Demon Extermination Unit hurled themselves forward to shield Jin Ha-gun.

Clang.

They drew their swords in unison.

The one who had entered without concealing his killing intent was Bi Sa-in.

The Thirteen Wolves of Sado also drew their swords at once and stepped forward.

The Demon Extermination Unit and the Thirteen Wolves of Sado faced off, blades aimed at one another.

Behind Bi Sa-in, Goeak and Gwangseom entered. The expressions of the Demon Extermination Unit’s warriors hardened. At a glance, they were masters beyond their own level, and the killing intent they exuded was so dense it was suffocating.

It was then.

Whoooooosh!

A strand of clear, refreshing energy blew in like the wind, pushing back the killing intent that had been encroaching upon them. It was the aura released by the Sword Immortal. As the killing intent was forced back, the faces of the Demon Extermination Unit’s warriors eased.

At that, Goeak and Gwangseom drew up even more internal energy and unleashed their killing intent. The Sword Immortal did not yield even before the combined pressure of the two.

As an invisible contest of wills unfolded between them, a taut tension filled the hall in an instant. It was a situation where, if someone were to drop even a dagger by mistake, a bloody battle might erupt.

Just as the atmosphere was growing increasingly vicious, Bi Sa-in turned and spoke respectfully to Goeak and Gwangseom.

“For now, I must exchange a few words with the commander over there. Why don’t you seniors settle your old feelings a little later?”

At that, the killing intent pouring from Goeak and Gwangseom subsided. Whether they wished it or not, they obeyed the young alliance leader’s orders without fail. The Sword Immortal also withdrew the aura he had released and stared steadily at the two masters of the unorthodox faction. They knew well who each other was. Their eyes exchanged such words.

—You were still alive?

—With evils overflowing in the world, I haven’t even the time to die.

But since they had come escorting important people, they did not continue their emotional clash.

Bi Sa-in stepped forward and said,

“I am Bi Sa-in, young alliance leader of the Sado Alliance.”

When he revealed his identity, the atmosphere in the hall froze.

Seo Baek-jung, in particular, trembled in fear. The person who absolutely must not appear had appeared.

And only then did he understand why the heads had not come.

‘They’ve all turned on me!’

At Bi Sa-in’s appearance, Seo Cheong retreated far away and prepared to flee at any moment.

Jin Ha-gun stepped forward and greeted him.

“I am Jin Ha-gun, commander of the Murim Alliance’s Demon Extermination Unit.”

It was the first meeting between the two. They looked at each other as if probing one another. In the future, when one became the leader of the Murim Alliance and the other the leader of the Sado Alliance, they would be lifelong archenemies.

“How is it that you have become an uninvited guest?”

Bi Sa-in smoothly deflected Jin Ha-gun’s rebuke.

“That is what I came to ask. Why did the invitation meant for us go to your side? I assume it must have been a mistake.”

“Unfortunately for you, it was no mistake. Sect Leader Seo has already decided to stand with the Murim Alliance.”

At that, Bi Sa-in looked at Seo Baek-jung and asked,

“Is that true?”

How could Seo Baek-jung dare say that it was?

Jin Ha-gun stepped in on behalf of the troubled man.

“See here. Just what do you think you’re doing, coming into someone else’s territory?”

A taut tension flowed between the two men.

Jin Ha-gun had no intention of yielding even an inch. The same was true of Bi Sa-in.

Both the Demon Extermination Unit and the Thirteen Wolves of Sado were tense. If a melee broke out, there could be great casualties.

It was then.

Creeeeak.

The doors slowly opened, and a new person appeared.

Everyone’s eyes turned that way.

Seo Baek-jung was startled out of his wits. Bi Sa-in’s arrival alone had been disastrous, yet another person who absolutely must not come had arrived.

Geom Mugeuk stepped inside, exuding demonic energy.

Along with him, all twelve of his guards entered as well.

The Demon Extermination Unit and the Thirteen Wolves of Sado, who had been facing off, split in half and confronted the Heavenly Demon Hall guards as well.

Behind Geom Mugeuk, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Fist Demon followed in.

At the appearance of the two Demon Venerables, even the Sword Immortal, Goeak, and Gwangseom flinched in surprise. They knew one another.

In an instant, Geom Mugeuk shrouded the place in demonic energy and spoke coldly.

“Since we’re talking about territory, let’s get our words straight. This is our territory.”

Chapter 273: This Drinking Party’s Righteous-Demonic War.
---------------------------------------------------------
With Geom Mugeuk’s appearance, the situation entered a new phase.

Bi Sain had expected Geom Mugeuk to come, so he was not greatly surprised, but Jin Hagun was startled.

‘So that man is Geom Mugeuk!’

The warriors who had entered earlier had shields and evil spirits emblazoned on their chests. That meant they were guards of the Heavenly Demon Hall. In that case, the two demonic practitioners of extraordinary bearing who had followed them in had to be Demon Supremes.

The old man carrying a great blade larger than his own torso must be the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, and that man who looked as if he had been forged from steel must be the Fist Demon. In particular, the moment he saw the Fist Demon’s face, his heart dropped. It was truly the first time he had ever seen a face so terrifying.

‘Even the Demonic Cult has appeared?’

Jin Hagun was flustered, but on the other hand, he understood. Since it had been the Demonic Cult that had tried to swallow the Cheonhwa Sect in the first place, of course they would not simply withdraw.

The person most shocked by Geom Mugeuk’s appearance was Seo Baekjung. Since he had used the power of the Murim Alliance to extract his son, the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult must have been very angry. And for such a man to appear here with Demon Supremes in tow meant?

Seo Baekjung had weathered all manner of hardships, but at this very moment, his heart shriveled.

‘One wrong move and I die.’

If the Young Alliance Leader of the Evil Dao Alliance or that Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult tried to kill him, would Jin Hagun risk his life to stop them? He might make the matter public and raise an issue over it, but that would all happen after Seo Baekjung was already dead.

Until now, he had walked the line between the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions well, but now he truly had to walk it well. That line was his lifeline.

Meanwhile, Seo Cheong was feeling confused. When Bi Sain had entered earlier, he had been filled with fear, but once Geom Mugeuk appeared as well, he was bewildered, thinking, ‘Just what kind of situation is this?’

Then his eyes met Geom Mugeuk’s. Geom Mugeuk’s expression seemed to be saying this.

I told you I’d be seeing you again soon, didn’t I? You stay right there.

At that, Seo Cheong bristled inwardly.

‘Don’t make me laugh!’

Seo Cheong, who had retreated into a corner, instead walked forward.

‘Even so, they won’t be able to do anything to me!’

He did not know how the current situation was unfolding. He had lived his life with the complacent thought that whenever a crisis came, ‘Something will work out.’

Rather, now that the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions were all gathered, he felt reassured. His instincts told him this: with this many people here, surely someone would protect him.

He did not know. That all these people had come to devour the Cheonhwa Sect. That this time, nothing would work out.

Geom Mugeuk walked toward Jin Hagun. The Demon-Extermination Unit grew tense and aimed their swords. The Heavenly Demon Hall guards also aimed their swords at them. It was a situation in which their subordinates were more tense than Geom Mugeuk and Jin Hagun themselves.

Geom Mugeuk split through the ranks of the guard warriors and walked forward.

“I am Geom Mugeuk, Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

Jin Hagun split through the warriors of the Demon-Extermination Unit and walked forward.

“I am Jin Hagun, captain of the Murim Alliance’s Demon-Extermination Unit.”

With only a few steps between them, the gazes of the two men tangled in the air.

A historic moment: the meeting between the certain next Heavenly Demon and the leading candidate to become the next leader of the Murim Alliance.

Jin Hagun was inwardly surprised. Geom Mugeuk was different from what he had imagined. Leaving aside the fact that he was young and handsome, his eyes were clear and deep. He did not feel evil at all. Suddenly, his younger sister’s words came to mind.

—What if he’s a demonic knight-errant?

Jin Hagun soon denied his sister’s words. It was a preconception brought on by appearance. He must never be deceived by looks.

Geom Mugeuk sensed that Jin Hagun closely resembled Jin Haryeong. Despite his young age, his eyes held chivalry and righteous energy. If one wished for the sake of murim, one should hope that Jin Hagun became the next alliance leader; if one dreamed of unifying murim, one should hope that Jin Hagun did not become the alliance leader.

Behind them, the war of nerves between the Demon-Extermination Unit and the guards lined up behind them was fierce. There was not the slightest retreat in the eyes with which they glared at one another.

Geom Mugeuk said to Jin Hagun,

“Rather than standing around like this, shall we sit down and discuss territory in earnest?”

At that, Jin Hagun turned around and walked to the table of wine. Then, as if to make a point, he sat in the seat of honor. He gestured with his hand for them to sit to his left and right.

At that sight, Geom Mugeuk smiled faintly. Not bad. Jin Hagun’s first impression passed in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes. If he had met the grandson of the Murim Alliance Leader and someone like Seo Cheong had come out, he would truly have felt quite displeased. But Jin Hagun had good eyes and good presence.

The first to move was Bi Sain. Bi Sain strode forward.

He did not sit in the seat Jin Hagun had indicated, but sat directly across from him.

And without a word, he stared at Jin Hagun. A quiet battle of wills had begun between the two.

At that sight, Geom Mugeuk’s smile deepened. That’s right. There was no way Bi Sain would meekly do as Jin Hagun wanted.

Jin Hagun may have lived his whole life as the protagonist, but here, at least, he would learn that he might become a supporting actor.

Geom Mugeuk sat in the seat Jin Hagun had offered. Naturally, he ended up sitting between Jin Hagun and Bi Sain as they faced off.

“There’s nothing more entertaining in the world than watching a fight, and a fight between the heirs of the orthodox and unorthodox factions? I’ve claimed the best seat.”

Even with Geom Mugeuk’s banter, the tension in the room did not ease.

Rather, as the three men sat close together, a war of nerves on the brink of eruption unfolded.

The Demon-Extermination Unit, the Thirteen Wolves of the Evil Dao, and the Heavenly Demon Hall guards stood lined up behind their respective masters, glaring at one another. It was only because the people who had come with them were such supreme experts, but even these men alone were truly elite warriors who knew how to fight. If a battle broke out, the place would become a sea of blood in an instant.

Jeokyeon was sending sound transmissions to the guards lined up with him.

—Do not be nervous!

—No matter what happens, we must protect the Young Cult Leader’s safety.

—Do not be afraid.

He continued sending transmissions, soothing them. In truth, they were words he was saying to himself as well. He had never imagined he would have to guard Geom Mugeuk in a situation like this. Truly, he was feeling it all over again: his master was the hardest leader in all murim to guard.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Fist Demon, the Sword Immortal, Goeak and Gwangseom stood naturally a short distance away. The initial tension and the tension after the Demon Supremes joined in were completely different. Though none of them showed it, they were ready to make a move at any moment.

The eyes of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Sword Immortal met.

“It has been a long time.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon greeted the Sword Immortal first. The Sword Immortal accepted his greeting.

“About ten years, has it been?”

“I believe it has been longer.”

The Sword Immortal usually loathed the unorthodox factions and the Demonic Cult, but the look in his eyes as he regarded the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was not that bad. It seemed as though there had been some connection between the two in the past.

“To think we would meet in a place like this.”

At the Sword Immortal’s words, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon replied,

“I am glad you look healthy.”

“You have grown even thinner.”

At that moment, Goeak stepped in.

“You are not even close, so why are you both pretending to be so friendly?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon said to him,

“You still have that habit of butting in without care.”

“Senior, you still have that habit of looking down on people.”

Goeak addressed the Blood Heaven Blade Demon as senior.

“Now that you know I am still the same, it would be best for you to keep quiet.”

Goeak snorted, but he could not bring himself to argue. Leaving martial prowess aside, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was even more difficult to deal with than the Sword Immortal. Indeed, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, murim’s spiciest old ginger, was showing why Geom Mugeuk had needed to call him this time.

Meanwhile, the Fist Demon stood with his two fists hanging at his sides, his face expressionless, and that alone made everyone there tense. It was not only because of his face. His presence was tremendous. The Fist Demon when seen ordinarily and the Fist Demon with enemies before him felt completely different.

Jin Hagun lifted the wine bottle and filled the cups. It was as if he were acting as the master of this place in Seo Baekjung’s stead. He had to deal with both the Demonic Cult and the Evil Dao Alliance, and he was trying not to be pushed back in momentum.

“I have heard many rumors about you, Young Cult Leader.”

Jin Hagun was especially wary of Geom Mugeuk. His opponent was a demonic practitioner. Not only that, he was the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult, said to be the greatest successor in its history, having even made the Demon Supremes his own people. Since he did not know what kind of scheme his opponent might pull, he was extremely tense.

“What sort of rumors were they?”

“Most of them were hard to believe.”

“Then what do you think, seeing me in person?”

Honestly, he had no grasp at all of what kind of person his opponent was.

Instead of answering, Jin Hagun raised his cup high. More than anyone, he knew that when one did not know, silence was the wise choice.

“Let us drink!”

The three men toasted and emptied their cups. It was truly the first drinking table of three men that would be spoken of again and again in the history of the jianghu.

As he filled the second cup, Jin Hagun asked,

“It seems the two of you are not meeting for the first time.”

He could tell from the way Geom Mugeuk and Bi Sain did not greet each other separately.

“That is right. We have met before.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Bi Sain and greeted him.

“Have you been well?”

He would not reveal that they had plotted this matter together, but he did not bother pretending this was their first meeting.

Bi Sain answered bluntly.

“I was well until you appeared.”

He reacted coldly for no reason because Jin Hagun was watching, but he was grateful to Geom Mugeuk. Thanks to him, he had been able to absorb fifteen small and mid-sized factions in Hunan into the Evil Dao Alliance. Of course, that did not mean he intended to yield the Cheonhwa Sect. They had agreed on fair competition, after all.

—I will not yield.

At Bi Sain’s sound transmission, Geom Mugeuk replied,

—I was planning to yield, though.

—How can you lie without so much as a change in expression?

—Was it that obvious?

Bi Sain turned his head away with an expression that said he was hopeless. The tension that had been taut between him and Jin Hagun immediately loosened as soon as he exchanged sound transmissions with Geom Mugeuk. If he had not known what kind of person Geom Mugeuk was, he might have taken that behavior as frivolous. But now he knew. The hardest person to deal with among all the masters here was Geom Mugeuk.

Meanwhile, sound transmissions were also passing between Jin Hagun and Seo Baekjung. At today’s gathering, everything would be decided not by the words that could be heard, but by those that could not.

—Do not be intimidated under any circumstances.

—I trust only you, Captain.

Contrary to his words, however, Seo Baekjung was inwardly uneasy. Now that the unorthodox factions and the Demonic Cult had come at him in earnest, he was even considering the possibility that he might have to change boats depending on how the situation flowed.

And finally, what had to come came.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Seo Baekjung, who was standing beside them, and said,

“Now, Sect Leader Seo, you should join us for a drink as well.”

Seo Baekjung sat beside Jin Hagun and received a cup of wine.

Bi Sain continued what he had been saying before Geom Mugeuk appeared.

“Sect Leader Seo, are you truly going to walk the same path as the Murim Alliance?”

Seo Baekjung still hesitated to answer.

The reason he had chosen the Murim Alliance was because of Baek Cheongyeong, the man in the bamboo hat and Jin Hagun’s master. It was not a relationship he could easily betray, but the same was true on this side. He could hardly say to the heir of the Evil Dao Alliance, “Yes, we betrayed you.”

Seo Baekjung glanced furtively at his rescuer, Jin Hagun.

Jin Hagun’s eyes said, There is no need to mind those people. Speak proudly.

Geom Mugeuk, who was observing the reactions of the two men, asked Jin Hagun,

“Is this your first time meeting Sect Leader Seo?”

“It is.”

He tried to show Seo Baekjung that he was confident before Geom Mugeuk and Bi Sain. But his answer gave Geom Mugeuk another question.

“How very strange. If this is your first meeting, what exactly did Sect Leader Seo trust in when he asked you for help?”

Geom Mugeuk and Bi Sain’s gazes turned toward Seo Baekjung.

Ordinarily, he should have said something along the lines of, “I have admired the orthodox factions from the beginning.”

But he could not predict how this situation would unfold. He was being careful with his words in case there came a need to attach himself to the Demonic Cult or the unorthodox factions. What if Jin Hagun grew angry because of this? It did not matter if he did. After all, all three of them wanted the Cheonhwa Sect. It was precarious, but the hilt of the blade was still in his hand.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk asked directly,

“Who was the person who served as the bridge between you two?”

This was the very point Geom Mugeuk was most interested in regarding this affair: he was asking who had planned it.

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, which struck the heart of the matter, Seo Baekjung and Jin Hagun gave no answer. They should have said that there was no such person, but the sudden question kept them from doing so.

‘There is definitely someone.’

It was the moment Geom Mugeuk’s conjecture became certainty.

‘Someone who knows both Jin Hagun and Seo Baekjung connected them.’

He had to uncover that man’s identity. Not merely because he had connected Jin Hagun and Seo Baekjung. It was because he possessed the audacity to confront the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Who in the world could he be?

Jin Hagun sensed that Seo Baekjung was wavering and immediately sent a voice transmission.

“—Sect Leader, you must trust us.”

“—I do trust you. However, as you can see, they show no signs of giving up easily.”

“—We will not give up, either.”

“—Give me your assurance.”

Seo Baekjung demanded.

Jin Hagun was in a situation where he could not abandon the Cheonhwa Gate, so he spoke decisively, as if making a proclamation.

“Now that the Cheonhwa Gate and this Alliance have joined hands, we will most certainly protect the Cheonhwa Gate. Any threat to the Cheonhwa Gate will be regarded as an attack on this Alliance.”

As if responding to that declaration, the martial artists of the Demon Annihilation Squad raised their fighting spirit.

Then Bi Sain spoke coldly.

“That is your thinking. I have yet to hear the answer from the Cheonhwa Gate Leader.”

As if they had been waiting, the Thirteen Wolves of the Demonic Path also raised their fighting spirit.

Bi Sain sent a voice transmission to Seo Baekjung.

“—All the sects that once followed you have turned away. Now, if trouble stirs in Honam, who will aid the Cheonhwa Gate? Do you think the leader of the Demon Annihilation Squad will remain here and protect you for the rest of his days? This is no threat; it is simply reality.”

How could Seo Baekjung be ignorant of that reality?

“—The best path is to walk alongside us.”

Seo Baekjung thought that, realistically, it might indeed be the wisest choice, and looked toward Geom Mugeuk.

There were three ropes. Two swayed before him, begging him to take hold. But the last rope lay far away.

Why do you not offer me yours?

As if reading that very thought, Geom Mugeuk smiled and spoke.

“I find the righteous-demonic battle at this banquet has grown interesting. So never speak of following our cult.”

At those words, every eye turned to Geom Mugeuk. All there knew full well that he would never abandon the Cheonhwa Gate so easily. Faint smiles tugged at the corners of Hyeolcheon Doma and Gwonma’s mouths.

Bi Sain felt a pang of inward unease.

‘Could it be?’

He was gripped by an ominous premonition—that when they rose from this gathering, Seo Baekjung would declare that he chose the Demonic Cult. A premonition that he would say he would follow the Demonic Cult, even against his will.

And so Geom Mugeuk’s first covert attempt to make that premonition reality began.

Geom Mugeuk sent a voice transmission to Jin Hagun.

“—Leader Jin, let me ask you one thing.”

Jin Hagun fixed his gaze on Geom Mugeuk with a look that said no ploy would work. But then came words he had never imagined.

“—Do you wish to become the Murim Alliance Leader?”

Jin Hagun was startled. He had never expected to hear such a question.

To the wide-eyed Jin Hagun, there came words that would shake him even more powerfully than before.

“—Do you know? At this very moment, you are giving up the position of Murim Alliance Leader of your own accord.”

Chapter 274: Not the Orthodox-Unorthodox War but the Orthodox-Demonic War.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Becoming the Martial Alliance Leader had been Jin Hagun's long-cherished dream.

He had grown up watching his grandfather since childhood. The words he had heard countless times from those around him were these:

"You must succeed your grandfather."

He had naturally believed he would become the Martial Alliance Leader when he came of age. But his grandfather had announced that he would pass the successor's position to one who possessed the ability and qualifications.

So he worked hard, and worked even harder. Becoming the Martial Alliance Leader was Jin Hagun's very life itself. He couldn't even imagine a life where he failed to become the Martial Alliance Leader.

But what? He was giving up on becoming the Martial Alliance Leader of his own accord?

It was the most shocking thing Jin Hagun had heard since birth.

Watching Jin Hagun's expression harden, Hyeolcheon Doma and Gwonma subtly exchanged glances. They could tell that Geom Mugeuk had sent some kind of voice transmission.

Because they too had experienced it, they could predict what kind of state Jin Hagun was falling into right now.

Bi Sain was also sitting across from him, so she saw that expression.

'No!'

Of course, that didn't mean she interrupted the conversation by butting in between the two of them.

But the anxious Seo Baekjung had no such courtesy.

Seo Baekjung sent a voice transmission to Jin Hagun.

"What are you two discussing so intently?"

He shouldn't have butted in like this, but Jin Hagun's expression had been ominous.

"Wait a moment."

At the curt reply, Seo Baekjung intuited that something was going wrong. Jin Hagun had told him to wait, but he couldn't.

"If the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult is pulling some kind of trick, you must not fall for it!"

At Seo Baekjung's voice transmission, Jin Hagun looked at him coldly. His imposing aura as the leader of the Demon Annihilation Squad, the Martial Alliance's elite organization, overwhelmed Seo Baekjung.

At that sharp reaction, Seo Baekjung could no longer interfere. His premonition was not good. His gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk. He hadn't been an easy opponent from the first time they met. Could Jin Hagun really handle that man? Seo Baekjung intuited that a variable had occurred.

Jin Hagun sent a voice transmission to Geom Mugeuk.

"You say you are giving up on the position of Alliance Leader of your own accord? What does that mean?"

He tried to maintain his composure as much as possible, but his voice was trembling.

"It means that if you obtain the Cheonhwamun, you cannot obtain the position of Martial Alliance Leader."

Jin Hagun's heart, which he had tried so hard to calm, began beating rapidly again. The words "Martial Alliance Leader" were like a weakness to him.

'This is a trick. I absolutely must not fall for it.' Jin Hagun drank the liquor placed before him. After steadying his heart in that way, he calmly asked:

"Tell me the reason."

Geom Mugeuk did not answer immediately. Everyone around them focused on the two. They all felt it—that a very important conversation was taking place between them.

Geomseon sent a voice transmission to Hyeolcheon Doma.

"As long as I am here, it would be best not to pull any futile tricks."

Geomseon seemed to feel some anxiety. They were such supreme experts that they could detect changes in emotion merely from the shifting currents in the air.

"This is an era where old men cannot intervene even if they want to. We have no choice but to watch."

At Hyeolcheon Doma's reply, Geomseon spoke in a stubborn tone.

"One must intervene when intervention is needed."

The two men's personalities clearly had similarities.

"Isn't that why our cult cannot strike the Righteous Faction?"

It was praise that held him in high regard—meaning they couldn't strike because he was there—so Geomseon smiled faintly.

"What are you so grateful to the juniors for, that you sacrifice yourself to this extent?"

"It is not sacrifice. It is living a righteous life."

"Do so while living your own life as well."

They were words that would have drawn a fierce reprimand had anyone else spoken them. But Hyeolcheon Doma was the exception.

"What now... I've lived this way my whole life, how could that life change easily?"

"It might change."

Hyeolcheon Doma's gaze naturally turned to Geom Mugeuk. Wondering what Hyeolcheon Doma's final words meant, Geomseon's gaze also turned toward Geom Mugeuk and Jin Hagun.

At this moment, Jin Hagun was pressing Geom Mugeuk.

"I'm telling you to tell me the reason!"

Then Geom Mugeuk stared at him and answered.

"The reason is something you know as well, isn't it?"

The moment he heard those words, Jin Hagun's heart sank. Geom Mugeuk was beginning to dig into that which he had deliberately buried and pretended not to know.

Jin Hagun's gaze passed over Geom Mugeuk and turned to one seated far away. It was none other than Seo Cheong.

He was the one who had killed an innocent person. Jin Hagun had been constantly worrying about how to deal with him. Even when Seo Baekjung first introduced his son, the reason Jin Hagun hadn't exchanged a single friendly word with him was because of that worry.

"What do you mean?"

He regretted it the moment he asked. He had pretended not to know in the hope that it might be a different reason.

'Damn it.'

He shouldn't have asked even if it were a different reason. He, who dreamed of becoming the Martial Alliance Leader, had pretended not to know to avoid the truth?

So even if it had been a different reason, he might have gotten away with it, but Geom Mugeuk struck precisely at his weakness.

"I speak of Seo Cheong."

Jin Hagun ground his molars without realizing it. 'Get a hold of yourself! You can't get entangled with him.' Unfortunately, this was not an opponent against whom such resolve would work.

"You came here knowing full well what crime he committed, did you not?"

"I..."

Jin Hagun forced himself to answer calmly.

"I intended to worry about his punishment after settling the matter of the Cheonhwamun."

"You will punish him later?"

Geom Mugeuk asked after a brief pause.

"Is that not something you must not do?"

Geom Mugeuk's words flew like a dagger and lodged deep. They were words he should have been the one to say.

'Your Demonic Cult, your Unorthodox Faction—isn't that something you must not do?'

Geom Mugeuk was feeling Jin Hagun's agitation. That was why he was hopeful. He was still young. He still had much to learn and much to experience. If he was someone who knew shame, then it was fine. It was fine to kick off the covers in one's youth.

Geom Mugeuk's voice transmission continued.

"The moment you obtain the Cheonhwamun, you will fail the test given by the Martial Alliance Leader."

"A test?"

At the word "test," Jin Hagun's mind snapped to attention.

"That is correct. This is the Alliance Leader's test."

"How do you know that?"

"Because I have met the Alliance Leader."

"Are you saying you know because you've met the Alliance Leader more times than I, his grandson, have?"

"Correct. The number of times I've met him is probably incomparable to yours. However, Young Lord Jin."

Geom Mugeuk gazed straight at him and asked.

"Until now, have you truly looked at your grandfather?"

Jin Hagun flinched for a moment. Truly looked at his grandfather?

'Of course I had.'

Those words didn't come out immediately. Because his grandfather... was such a difficult person for him.

"What are you trying to say?"

Jin Hagun's voice rose slightly. His composure was already shattered, and he was agitated.

"I mean that because he is someone close, you cannot see him properly. At least, that was the case for me."

Geom Mugeuk conveyed honestly what he had felt. Because he knew that even if he employed all manner of rhetorical techniques, in the end, what moved an opponent's heart was sincerity.

"Until recently, I could not see my father properly. I couldn't see him because I was afraid, and because he was someone I could see at any time, I couldn't see him. Only recently have I been trying to see him properly. That is why I say this. Even if you look a hundred times, a thousand times, if you do not see properly, you may know less than someone who has seen but once."

Their gazes tangled in midair.

Jin Hagun could not deny Geom Mugeuk's words. But he couldn't blindly believe them either. The thought occurred to him that perhaps his grandfather was not thinking such things, and he might simply be falling for one of Geom Mugeuk's tricks. He was confused.

"You are suspicious. How can you be confident that these words are not tricks to seize the Cheonhwamun?"

Then Geom Mugeuk struck his vital point once more.

"Then why are you looking at me?"

"What do you mean?"

"If you are suspicious, you should look at your grandfather. Is that not where you must find the answer?"

Suddenly, Jin Hagun recalled when he had gone to greet his grandfather before leaving for this place.

Now that he thought about it, his grandfather had not said a single word about the Cheonhwamun. Not that they must obtain it, nor how to conduct themselves regarding it.

He had simply told him to return safely. That gaze with which he had looked at him.

Was that truly a gaze that would rejoice at obtaining the Cheonhwamun without punishing the unjust one?

No.

Perhaps he had known all along. He had merely pretended not to. Because it was his master who had proposed it for him. Could it be that his master had spoken the words he himself had wanted to speak?

'Let us achieve a great feat and become the successor!'

Jin Hagun's face flushed hot. Even though he pretended not to know, even though he used his master as an excuse, he had known. That he had done it because he wanted to.

"The person who connected you and that Cheonhwamun leader does not wish for you to become the Alliance Leader."

Jin Hagun's expression instantly hardened completely.

Through that reaction, Geom Mugeuk was able to learn one fact.

'He is someone close to Jin Hagun.'

It was without a doubt someone closer to Jin Hagun than Seo Baekjung.

Jin Hagun trusted his master. He revered and followed him above anyone else.

He had believed his master proposed this matter to help him become the Alliance Leader.

'I must trust my master! I must not fall for sowing discord!'

Bi Sain was watching Jin Hagun's expression change from moment to moment.

What on earth had been said to throw Jin Hagun, whom she had just met, into such passion?

Well, if she thought about herself, the answer came out. If she thought about how her own emotions had changed after meeting Geom Mugeuk. What was truly taking place at this drinking gathering was not a clash between righteous and evil, but a clash between the Righteous and the Demonic.

Bi Sain sent a voice transmission to Geom Mugeuk.

"You are the number one villain in all the martial world!"

"The voice transmissions are getting crossed, so send yours later!"

"No! Please get them crossed and misunderstand it terribly."
