Bi Sain was astonished that she was saying such things. She had never imagined that someone like herself could exchange such jokes with another person. She had thought that with this hideous scar, she could not share sincere conversations with anyone.

Meanwhile, Seo Cheong sent a voice transmission to Seo Baekjung.

"Why is it so quiet?"

"They are conversing through voice transmission."

"Someday I will take revenge on that Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult. Father, please take revenge for me!"

Seo Baekjung found his son pathetic for being unable to read the atmosphere. He didn't even say he would take revenge himself, but "please take revenge for me." Compared to those three successors, he was all the more pathetic.

But the fact that he had raised him this way was ultimately his own responsibility.

"Do not act rashly and stay quiet."

They say the position makes the person. He had thought that if he created a great position and passed it down, his son would become someone befitting it. It was a complacent thought. Even if it was just a portion of the effort put into raising the Cheonhwamun—no, raising a child was an endeavor that took even greater effort than all of that.

The price of avoiding that responsibility was speaking before his eyes right now.

"Should I quietly slip away just in case?"

Seo Baekjung answered with a sigh.

"It is already too late. So stay put."

Meanwhile, the voice transmission between Geom Mugeuk and Jin Hagun continued.

"Very well, let us say you are right. Then why are you telling me this? To seize the Cheonhwamun?"

"Of course, that is part of the reason, but there is a greater reason than that."

"What is it?"

"It is because I wished for you to be someone who keeps to principles."

A cold aura erupted from Jin Hagun's body. He did not want to hear talk of principles from a demon.

As he raised his momentum, those around him tensed simultaneously.

The Demon Annihilation Squad raised their internal energy, and the Heavenly Demon Hall guards and the Sado Sipsamnang also raised their momentum, preparing for a counterattack.

On the other hand,Geom Mugeuk was exceedingly calm.

"Later, when either I become the Cult Leader or you become the Alliance Leader, thousands or tens of thousands of lives will hang on our decisions. At that time, I hope we do not make the wrong decisions. You, me, and that Young Leader Bi over there. I hope we are people who keep to principles."

The aura that had surged out from Jin Hagun subsided. He could feel it. That Geom Mugeuk was speaking from the heart. His eyes were saying this. Become a good Alliance Leader. And so, let us not wage war in our era.

'I've lost.'

It was the single phrase that rose in Jin Hagun's heart.

Whether Geom Mugeuk's words were false or sincere, it was his complete defeat. He had to admit it. Whether Geom Mugeuk was a conman or a true Demonic Hero, he was at least someone more outstanding than himself. Someone he would have difficulty handling.

'I must work harder. Ten times, a hundred times harder than now!'

For the day when he himself became Alliance Leader, he had to ensure that the Murim Alliance would not be taken from him by that Geom Mugeuk before his eyes.

Jin Ha-gun spoke calmly.

—I will become a man who upholds principles. Not because you want that of me. Because I have always wanted to become such a person.

—I believe you will.

—Are you mocking me?

—Mockery should be reserved for someone like that Seo Cheong over there. Should it not be directed at Seo Baekjung, who is trying to protect a man like that simply because he is his son? Is that not why we exist? If not us, then who could restrain the children of great sects like Cheonhwamun?

—You really are……

It was a complete defeat to the very end.

Jin Ha-gun’s gaze suddenly turned toward Bisa-in, who sat across from him.

Their eyes met. So absorbed had he been in Geom Mugeuk that he had not even realized Bisa-in had been looking at him like this.

Without a word, Bisa-in filled Jin Ha-gun’s empty cup with wine.

The feeling was somewhat different now from when he had first sat down opposite him so provocatively.

Jin Ha-gun drank the wine Bisa-in had poured for him. Who would have thought that wine poured by the successor of the Evil Faction would be so comforting?

Bisa-in was not the only one whose demeanor had changed.

Seo Baekjung was looking at him with a strange gaze. It was as though that gaze were asking:

‘Surely you haven’t been won over by that Demon Cult bastard and are planning to withdraw your hand from us?’

Just then, Geom Mugeuk’s sound transmission came flying in.

—Are you worried about your promise with the Cheonhwamun Master?

How quick he was to read the room. Yes, he was worried. Until moments ago, he had been loudly declaring that they could rely on him alone, and now to say, My mind has changed, made it hard for him to open his mouth. Moreover, they were not alone; all these people were watching, which made it even harder.

—I will resolve it so that you won’t be put in a difficult position. Would that be all right?

The words “I don’t want that” rose to Jin Ha-gun’s throat. In terms of momentum, he no longer wanted to be swayed by Geom Mugeuk. But he had to withdraw his hand from Cheonhwamun. Only then would he have something to say to his grandfather when he returned.

Just how did he intend to resolve it without putting him in a difficult position? He was far too curious about that method.

The very moment Jin Ha-gun’s head gave a slight nod, Geom Mugeuk ended the long sound transmission and opened his mouth.

“Now, now. Let us have a drink and ease the tension.”

They all drank together. Everyone was curious as to what conclusion Geom Mugeuk and Jin Ha-gun’s sound-transmission conversation had reached.

Setting down his cup, Geom Mugeuk said as though it were nothing significant.

“Originally, this matter was not something that should have grown this large. As you all know, this affair began because our Sect Master Seo wished to protect his son, who had committed murder several times. I understand, as it is the bond between parent and child. However, considering that those who died were innocent people……”

At that moment, someone cut off Geom Mugeuk’s words.

“What do you mean by that?”

It was the man who had lived his entire life for good deeds and righteousness—the Sword Immortal.

Chapter 275: I Told You Not to Choose This Sect
-----------------------------------------------
Naturally, it was something said for Geomseon to hear.

Geom Muguk rose from his seat and greeted Geomseon with a respectful cupped-fist salute.

“I was unable to pay my respects properly earlier. It is an honor to meet you.”

Geomseon sized up Geom Muguk with a sharp gaze. He had done so when they first met as well, but once again, he admired Geom Muguk’s eyes and bearing.

*To think his eyes are so clear despite having trained in the demonic arts? Truly astonishing.*

Geomseon’s gaze turned to Hyeolcheon Doma, who stood behind Geom Muguk. He remembered what Hyeolcheon Doma had said earlier.

—It may yet change.

Had those words, spoken as if he had changed his life, been about this Young Cult Leader?

Geomseon asked again in a low yet grave tone.

“Repeat what you said earlier.”

“I had assumed you were unaware. Had you known, you would not have tolerated it.”

Geomseon had only accompanied Jin Hagun at the request of Martial Alliance Leader Jin Paecheon to protect him; he did not know what business Jin Hagun had come out for. He had not been interested in the first place.

“Do you mean what you said earlier is true?”

“That is correct. That man there, Seo Cheong, murdered a passing traveler simply because he wished to kill, and he also killed a martial artist of our cult. There were other victims as well. I happened to be in Honam and was about to punish Seo Cheong when the Martial Alliance stepped in to stop me.”

Geomseon turned a cold, settled gaze toward Jin Hagun and asked.

“Lord Jin, are those words true?”

If he answered yes, given Geomseon’s temperament, it would not matter whether he was the grandson of the Martial Alliance Leader or the Martial Alliance Leader himself—Geomseon would give a ruthless order.

Jin Hagun could see Geom Muguk’s intent. He meant to use Geomseon to escape the situation.

“It is true.”

In an instant, rage surged from Geomseon.

Jin Hagun quickly added.

“However, there is one fact that was left out.”

Jin Hagun looked at Seo Cheong and spoke.

“I, too, was about to give Seo Cheong a fitting punishment for his crimes.”

Jin Hagun realized. This was the most natural way to cut ties with Cheonhwamun.

“What punishment?”

“I intended to confine him in the Noeok. He would have been imprisoned for life.”

Geomseon looked at Jin Hagun with a doubtful gaze. It was possible Jin Hagun was hastily making excuses because Geomseon had intervened.

Jin Hagun met Geomseon’s gaze unflinchingly. Truthfully, he might or might not have done so. But he chose to believe in himself. He would have punished him somehow.

Only then did Geomseon’s expression relax.

“Yes, I should have known you would do that.”

Geomseon put his weight behind Jin Hagun because he believed him. He had watched Jin Hagun grow up from a young age. He knew better than anyone that the boy had lived diligently and was upright.

“I believe you.”

“I will strive further so as not to disappoint you.”

Jin Hagun considered it fortunate that matters had not progressed toward absorbing Cheonhwamun. Thinking back now, he had truly been blinded by greed.

Of course, this did not mean Geomseon’s anger had vanished. Only its target had changed.

Geomseon’s gaze turned toward Seo Cheong. He should have rushed over to Geomseon upon being looked at, but Seo Cheong stood frozen in place, terrified.

Seo Baekjung called out in a loud voice.

“Why are you just standing there instead of coming here?”

Geomseon was already angry. If his mood was further provoked, one could lose their life on this very spot.

“Hurry up and come here!”

Seo Baekjung shouted abruptly and sent a voice transmission.

—Do as Geomseon says. Come here and beg for forgiveness as though I died!

—Please save me, Father! I can’t go to the Noeok! I’ll die if I go there!

—Shut up! This is no time to indulge in childish whining!

When Seo Baekjung exploded in anger, Seo Cheong walked toward Geomseon with a face full of fear.

Seo Baekjung’s urgent voice transmission flew to Jin Hagun.

—Please mediate!

—Now that Geomseon has intervened, there is nothing even I can do. As the Sect Leader knows, is he not the Martial Alliance Leader’s sworn brother and a man who has walked the path of righteousness his whole life?

—Surely you won’t let this go? Will that man simply overlook this matter?

Seo Baekjung mentioned Jin Hagun’s master.

Indeed, his master was the part that weighed most heavily on Jin Hagun’s mind.

But with Geomseon having stepped in, and having already resolved to cut ties with Cheonhwamun, Jin Hagun believed his master would surely understand his decision.

—Even he cannot go against Elder Geomseon’s will.

In the meantime, Seo Cheong stood before Geomseon.

Geomseon asked him with a cold gaze.

“Is what I heard from the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult earlier true?”

Seo Cheong hesitated, not knowing how to answer. His gaze once again turned to Seo Baekjung.

Had he been innocent, he would have denied it and complained of his grievances. This action alone was tantamount to admitting everything he had done.

Seo Baekjung stepped forward on his behalf.

“It is an incident still under investigation; the truth has not yet come to light.”

He was trying somehow to escape this moment. He expected that Jin Hagun would feel sorry toward him and answer accordingly.

But the one Geomseon asked was Geom Muguk.

“Young Cult Leader, are this man’s words true?”

“They are not. All his sins have already been brought to light.”

Thereupon, Geomseon berated Seo Baekjung with a cold gaze.

“Dare you speak falsehood to me?”

For Geomseon, even imprisoning Seo Cheong in the Noeok was unsatisfactory. He had made it his life’s work to execute those who killed the innocent, regardless of their status.

Seo Baekjung lowered his head as if admitting fault and sent a voice transmission.

—Hurry up and kneel to beg!

Only then did Seo Cheong fall to his knees. He was terrified.

*Anything but the Noeok!*

Seo Cheong pleaded with Geomseon.

“I was drunk and made a mistake. I cannot remember what happened that day. Please forgive me.”

In an instant, Geomseon’s expression grew even colder. Not a word of wrongdoing came out—only the word “mistake.” And to blame it on alcohol?

As the saying goes, to see one is to know ten. Geomseon could immediately grasp what kind of person Seo Cheong was.

“It was a mistake?”

“Yes, it was a mistake.”

“Then why did you kill the others?”

“That is….”

He could think of no excuse. He raised his head and pleaded.

“Please forgive me just this once.”

“I am asking you why you killed them.”

At the scathing rebuke, Seo Cheong’s mind went blank.

In that dazed, blank state, a single emotion welled up from deep within Seo Cheong’s heart. The one he had used most often in his life toward others: rage.

Seo Cheong was furious. Never had anyone pushed him into a corner like this.

In the next moment, he shouted.

“Because I wanted to kill them!”

In an instant, silence fell over the place.

“Because killing people was thrilling.”

Seo Cheong raised his head, looked around, and shouted even louder.

“You all know that feeling, don’t you? You’ve killed far more than I have! Why are you doing this to me alone!”

Everyone there simply looked at him with cold eyes.

The one who rushed to him was Seo Baekjung.

Slap!

Seo Baekjung ruthlessly struck Seo Cheong’s cheek.

“Shut up!”

“Why are you hitting me? Did I say anything wrong?”

He should have known he was being hit to save him, but the excited Seo Cheong saw nothing before his eyes.

“Why is that old man trying to punish me? This is not the Martial Alliance’s territory, but the Demonic Cult’s, is it not? If I am to be punished, it should be by them!”

Slap! Slap!

Seo Baekjung struck Seo Cheong mercilessly.

Whom could he blame? It was his own fault for raising his son this way.

Seo Baekjung could feel it. Geomseon’s fury had reached its peak. He realized that if things continued this way, Geomseon might kill Seo Cheong.

He quickly sent a voice transmission to Bi Sain.

—We will stand together with the Demon Path Alliance.

Bi Sain did not reply immediately to his desperate message. Because he now held the upper hand.

Bi Sain admired Geom Muguk. To think he brought in Geomseon and drove matters to this point? Looking at the result alone, it might seem like something anyone could think of, but this was not something just anyone could accomplish.

While Seo Baekjung sent a voice transmission to draw in the Demon Path Alliance, unable to endure the wait, Seo Cheong stirred up trouble.

He was angry at his father. He misunderstood that the man who should have saved him by any means was keeping his mouth shut to save himself alone. And he was even hitting him.

With no affection or trust between parent and child in normal times, in this extreme situation, only extreme thoughts came to mind.

Driven by the misunderstanding and fear that his father had abandoned him, Seo Cheong blurted out something he should never have said.

“Father, you killed people too, didn’t you?”

Flustered, Seo Baekjung quickly spoke to Geomseon.

“He is frightened and spouting nonsense; please pay him no mind.”

Seo Baekjung turned to Seo Cheong and scowled.

—Shut up! Do you really want to kill your own father, too?

He spoke in haste, but it was a slip of the tongue. Seo Cheong interpreted those words as, “If someone has to die, you die alone!” Seo Cheong shrieked like a madman.

“You poisoned Sadogeom and buried him in the bamboo grove behind the mountain, didn’t you? You killed the Honam Samrang too, and Myeoljeolgeom!”

Silence arrived in an instant.

Names that should not have been spoken came out. The Honam Samrang and Myeoljeolgeom were figures who walked the line between the orthodox and unorthodox, but Sadogeom was a martial artist belonging to the Demon Path Alliance.

Seo Baekjung looked at Bi Sain with a panicked face.

—So the missing Sadogeom was buried behind your mountain, Sect Leader Seo.

—It is a misunderstanding.

—There is no need for excuses. Sadogeom was not one of my people anyway.

—Then will you join hands with us?

—Regrettably, that is difficult. No matter how unorthodox we are, do we not have our pride?

They could not accept them knowing they had killed an unorthodox martial artist. There were more than a few watching eyes.

Geom Muguk had rolled a ripe pumpkin straight to his feet, yet it tumbled away and slipped through his grasp.

In the end, Seo Baekjung clung to the side farthest from himself.

“We will join hands with the Heavenly Demon Cult. Please accept us.”

Seo Baekjung did not even send a voice transmission. He had no leisure to coordinate the situation through voice transmission now. His son was one thing, but now his own life was in danger as well. The only place he could rely on was the Cult.

At that sight, Bi Sain let out a sigh, thinking, *So it comes to this, after all.* When Geom Muguk had jokingly told him never to choose the Cult, Bi Sain had a premonition that it might end up this way.

And indeed, it happened.

“Did I not clearly say I did not want that? I told you not to choose our Cult.”

“Please accept us.”

“I said no! Go to the Martial Alliance that you favor so much.”

“Please!”

“The Lesser Alliance Leader of the Demon Path Alliance may have a fearsome face, but he is a good man. Go to him.”

“Please accept us into the Divine Cult! I beg you!”

Seo Baekjung grabbed Geom Muguk’s pant leg and pleaded.

“This is troublesome.”

It did not deviate from Bi Sain’s prediction in the slightest.

In the end, pretending he could not win against him, Geom Muguk spoke nonchalantly.

“Very well, but there is one condition.”

“Speak it.”

“Your son is bound for the Noeok. As long as Cheonhwamun’s loyalty does not falter, his execution will be postponed.”

It was not forgiveness. Life in the Heavenly Demon Cult’s Noeok would bring agony greater than death.

At those words, Seo Cheong shrieked and began thrashing about again.

“Save me! Why me! I only killed a few worthless people, so why me! Father, please save me!”

He had lived his whole life doing as he pleased, but now was the moment he would taste the true terror of the world.

“You hypocrites! You kill people left and right, so why can’t I kill anyone!”

Seo Baekjung let out a sigh. His son looked as though he were shouting the words a grim reaper whispered in his ear.

In the end, Seo Cheong poured curses upon Seo Baekjung as well.

“What father in the world abandons his child! And you dare lead a sect, you villain!”

It was the moment the bond between the two was completely severed.

Seo Baekjung thought that this might be the better path for Cheonhwamun. Cheonhwamun would continue to operate well, and his son was alive, at any rate. That was enough.

Seo Baekjung composed his heart in that way.

Then Gwangseom, who had not uttered a single word until now, strode up and called out to Seo Baekjung.

“Sect Leader Seo.”

The moment Seo Baekjung turned toward him carelessly.

Swish!

Shhk.

Gwangseom cut him down with a single stroke. It was such a swift sword that his blade was not even visible.

No one had thought he would kill Seo Baekjung, so everyone was startled. In particular, Seo Cheong, his son, was engulfed in shock. Terrified that he might be next, he sank to the spot, his face turning ashen.

Gwangseom bowed his head to Bi Sain and spoke briefly.

“Sadogeom was my sworn younger brother. I will gladly accept punishment for acting without orders.”

Even Geom Muguk had not known this fact. When the indifferent heavens turned their gaze to the world, there were never any gaps through which to escape.

Geomseon, who had been watching this scene, sent a voice transmission to Hyeolcheon Doma.

—Did you create this situation?

He had thought Hyeolcheon Doma had been sending voice transmissions and setting plans throughout today’s gathering. To Geomseon, Hyeolcheon Doma was remembered as such a person.

—I wish that were the case, but….

Hyeolcheon Doma was not a man to lie about such matters.

Geomseon’s gaze turned to Geom Muguk.

—I shall have to live long.

He realized belatedly that even he had moved according to Geom Muguk’s will.

—If you meddle so much, you won’t hear good things from the younger folk.

—It is fine.

And so, Cheonhwamun was resolved.

With Seo Baekjung dead and Seo Cheong imprisoned in the Noeok, the new leader of Cheonhwamun would now be one who followed the Heavenly Demon Cult.

Everyone’s gaze naturally focused on Geom Muguk.

Today, Geom Muguk had accomplished everything. He had absorbed Cheonhwamun and exacted punishment for Seo Cheong’s crimes. Standing there with a faint smile and no tension, he now felt like a different person.

They say rumors are nine parts exaggeration out of ten, but at least the people gathered here would shake their heads and say this: that Geom Muguk is the remaining one part.

Jin Hagun and Bi Sain slowly walked up to Geom Muguk and stood facing each other in a circle.

Just as their meeting had been, it was the moment the first parting of three successors—unprecedented in the history of the martial world—was about to begin.

Chapter 276: Give Your Mouth a Rest
-----------------------------------
The three of them looked at one another.

To be exact, Jin Hagun and Bisain were looking at Geommugeuk.

The look in Jin Hagun's eyes toward Geommugeuk had changed from when they first exchanged greetings.

He suddenly recalled what his younger sibling had said before leaving.

"You must look at him without prejudice when dealing with him. Don't think, 'Since he's a demonic person, he would be like this.' He is not the kind of person you think he is, Brother."

He could now clearly understand why his younger sibling had said those words. Geommugeuk was not the person he had imagined. Jin Hagun felt curiosity toward the man before his eyes, who might become his lifelong rival.

Right then, someone spoke.

"...It's unjust."

Everyone's gaze turned toward the speaker.

Seocheong was sitting collapsed on the floor, looking this way. Though blood flowing from Seo Baekjung's body had soaked his buttocks, he was half out of his mind.

"I'm telling you it's unjust."

Until he was shouting wildly earlier, it had been fine. It had been refreshing. You hypocrites! Didn't you kill too?!

But that was all. The moment it ended, an intense emptiness arrived. No one was paying attention to him. It felt as if the fact that he had shouted had never happened.

He had always lived as the protagonist.

Yet now, not a single person gathered in this banquet hall was paying him any attention.

At a time when he should have been raging at his father's death and feeling terror at the fact that he would be imprisoned in an underground dungeon for life, he was writhing in a victim complex of being ignored and abandoned.

"So save me."

Geommugeuk was gazing at him with eyes devoid of any emotion. If he had been embracing his father's corpse and pouring out curses in this direction, he might have felt a shred of sympathy.

But Seocheong was a man who thought only of himself until the very last moment.

When Geommugeuk gestured with his eyes, two guard martial artists suppressed his internal energy and dragged him outside. Since they had even suppressed his vocal acupoints to prevent him from screaming, he was dragged out helplessly. He would soon be dragged to the hellish underground prison of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult's main compound through the Honam Branch demonic people.

Geommugeuk did not utter a single word to Seocheong, nor did he acknowledge his words. He paid not even the slightest trace of attention.

He would simply cast him into hell without placing any meaning on him.

This was Geommugeuk's unwavering attitude when dealing with villains who deserved death.

From that sight, Jin Hagun could somewhat feel what kind of person Geommugeuk was. Had it been him, he would surely have said something—that because of him his father had died, he should live his life in reflection. But Geommugeuk completely ignored him without even casting a cold gaze. That felt even more frightening and cruel.

Jin Hagun felt as though he had properly tasted the flavor of Murim today. It was an extremely spicy taste, but not an unpleasant one. It was a taste he wanted to savor again.

At the same time, he felt such desire: that next time, he wanted to be the one cooking and properly show the taste.

"I will not forget what happened today."

Though it might have sounded like a vow of vengeance, it was an expression of gratitude toward Geommugeuk. If it hadn't been for him, he would have fallen out of his grandfather's favor.

Geommugeuk answered with a gentle gaze and a smile.

"Become a good Alliance Chief."

Jin Hagun decided to believe only half of the sincerity contained in that gaze. He had never heard of a successor to the Demonic Cult advising someone to become a good Alliance Chief.

The Demon Annihilation Squad and Heavenly Demon Pavilion guards standing behind them, and the Sado Sipsamrang as well, were looking at one another. They sensed that this meeting was not the end. At what moment might they meet again?

Even at the final moment of parting, Geommugeuk's banter was not missing.

"It's a shame to part; let's go have a drink."

But Jin Hagun pretended not to hear and bid farewell to Bisain.

The silent drink Bisain had poured him earlier had been a great comfort.

"Next time, I will pour you a drink."

At his words, Bisain slightly nodded. Would a day truly come when he would receive a drink from a successor of the Righteous Faction? Perhaps it might. For ever since meeting that Geommugeuk, unimaginable things kept happening.

"Then let us meet again."

Jin Hagun strode out first. As the Demon Annihilation Squad tried to follow him out, Geommugeuk spoke to them.

"Well then, send your leader on his way and let us at least drink among ourselves. Let's talk with our guards while drinking. How much is the monthly pay, whether it comes out on time, what the mission environment is like. Aren't you curious about such things?"

The Demon Annihilation Squad members made bewildered expressions as they followed Jin Hagun out. Someone among them unknowingly let out a "pfft" before hurriedly straightening his face. The Demon Annihilation Squad, the Sado Sipsamrang, and the Heavenly Demon Pavilion guards drinking together in one place? Was such a thing even possible?

The last person to leave from the Righteous Faction was Geomseon.

Hyeolcheon Doma greeted him first.

"Let us meet again. Take care of your health."

"Don't worry. I now have a reason to live long."

"I told you I don't like meddling in the affairs of juniors."

"You should put on some weight."

Geomseon glanced fleetingly at the reason he had to live long, then left that place.

Geommugeuk spoke to Bisain.

"Now that all the stuffy Righteous Faction people have left, let us men have a drink!"

But Bisain also walked out pretending not to hear.

Bisain knew. That Geommugeuk had tried to give Cheonhwa Sect to him. If only Seocheong hadn't acted out at the last moment. Yes, how could one beat a lucky bastard?

Still, he had obtained fifteen martial sects in the Honam region during this excursion, achieving a great merit for the Alliance.

"Good grief, I said have a drink before you go. How heartless!"

Despite Geommugeuk's shout coming from behind, Bisain strode out of that place. There was no need to say thank you. Because Geommugeuk would appear before him again someday. He would show a back that looked like it wanted to be pushed off, and sitting on that cliff, he would surprise him again. Bisain even looked forward to that moment. He would repay this debt then.

Behind them, the Sado Sipsamrang followed, and Go'aek and Gwangseom walked out. The two experts as well watched Geommugeuk carefully as they left, rather than Hyeolcheon Doma or Gwonma. Everyone was feeling it—that the one who had created today's result was Geommugeuk.

After even they left, Geommugeuk laughed heartily and said,

"As expected! I have only our Demonic Lords."

But Hyeolcheon Doma and Gwonma were not in that spot. The two were already walking toward the door.

"Where are you going?"

"We have to go since the work is done."

"Wait, is everyone in on this? Why are you all like this to me?"

Geommugeuk quickly ran to block their path.

The guards moved accordingly and took formation. Samho looked at Jeokyeon and conveyed with his eyes the words he had said before.

—He is truly not easy to serve.

Jeokyeon also answered with his eyes.

—Isn't that why it's interesting?

Even though he displayed a dignity that other successors, and even Geomseon, could not recklessly approach, when he joked around like that, he felt like a completely different person.

Geommugeuk spoke to the two Demonic Lords.

"Whew, I spoke so much I thought my mouth would die from the pain."

It had truly been a Great War of Demonic Rule that started with words and ended with words.

Gwonma clenched his fist.

"Thanks to that mouth, there was no need to use this fist."

However, Gwonma's blunt eyes contained praise that said, "You handled it well."

"You trusted in that fist and chattered away to your heart's content."

Hyeolcheon Doma tried to leave through the door.

"Let that mouth rest now too. We're leaving."

"No, why does someone so idle in the world keep trying to leave?"

"I have to teach Daeryong, and I have to read books too. I'm busy as well."

Then Gwonma added his reason.

"I also have late-night training."

Geommugeuk grabbed the two of them.

"How heartless, leaving immediately! You came a long way, so stay a bit longer before you go. Who knows? An opportunity to use that fist to your heart's content might arise."

At those meaningful words, Gwonma asked.

"What is going on?"

"Let's go, I'll tell you on the way."

Geommugeuk squeezed between the two to prevent them from going anywhere, linked arms, and walked out together.

"You have to travel around diligently while you're young, even a year younger. What will you do if you already prefer staying home? Later you won't be able to travel even if you want to!"

Seeing the rare sight of him walking out with the two fearsome Demonic Lords on either side, the guards also left that place.

* * *

We returned to the Honam Branch.

I immediately dismissed all the guards to rest.

If I were to die even with Hyeolcheon Doma and Gwonma at the Honam Branch, I would die anyway even with the guards present.

The guards withdrew and enjoyed personal free time for the first time in a while. Those who wanted to sleep slept, those who wanted to drink drank, and those who wanted to train in martial arts trained.

I had a modest drinking session with the two Demonic Lords.

Though the two seemed like an unlikely match, they subtly got along well. Although Hyeolcheon Doma looked like a fussy old man, he had a manly disposition, and that suited Gwonma's personality well.

I emptied the first cup refreshingly with the two Demonic Lords.

"Thank you for coming like this. Thanks to you both, things went smoothly."

They were truly people who became strength by their mere presence. They had now become people to whom I could say anything and rely on completely.

"So what do you plan to do now?"

Hyeolcheon Doma set down his cup and asked.

"I have to go to the Gangseo Branch."

Because meeting with the Honam Branch leader and the Gangseo Branch leader was the purpose of this excursion.

"The Gangseo Branch leader's heart must be pounding."

Since the Honam Branch leader is dead, and the head of Cheonhwa Sect, the representative martial sect of this place, is also dead.

"If he has committed no crime, there is no reason to be uneasy."

"But he will still be uneasy, won't he? To hear that someone bringing a blood storm is coming."

"It's unjust. Where is there a person who loves peace as much as I do?"

I spoke while pouring drinks for the two again.

"But there is a matter that must be handled before going. That is why I wished for you two to stay."

I informed the two about the mastermind behind this matter.

"There is someone who connected the deceased head of Cheonhwa Sect with Jin Hagun. The head of Cheonhwa Sect asked him for help, and he immediately moved the Murim Alliance."

"Then he must be someone with power."

Having instinctively sensed he was someone hiding his claws, Hyeolcheon Doma's eyes flashed with strange light. It meant this matter might not be a simple incident that started from the Honam Branch leader's corruption.

"When I probed Jin Hagun earlier, I realized he is very close to him."

"The chances of him being an insider of the Murim Alliance are high."

"Yes, that is highly likely."

Hyeolcheon Doma immediately discerned my intention.

"You intend to capture him."

"At the very least, I must find out who it is. Not only because he poses a threat to me. It concerns me that such a person is around Jin Hagun. In other words, it means someone who could manipulate the future Murim Alliance Chief."

Hyeolcheon Doma and Gwonma nodded simultaneously.

"How do you plan to find out?"

"That is what you must tell me, Elder. Why else would I have invited you to such a distant place? I brought you here to hear what to do at times like this."

"Trying to work an old man to death. Well done."

But contrary to his words, Hyeolcheon Doma looked pleased. Was not the moment of being acknowledged by someone and confirmed as a necessary existence always enjoyable?

After pondering briefly, Hyeolcheon Doma raised a question.

"But why did he go this far?"

"What do you mean?"

"Why did he step forward mobilizing even Jin Hagun for this matter? Was he that close to the head of Cheonhwa Sect?"

"According to Tongcheon Pavilion's investigation, there was no Murim Alliance insider who was that close to him."

"Then why? It would become Jin Hagun's merit, not his own, anyway. And the benefit would all go to the Murim Alliance."

Hyeolcheon Doma's point was sharp. I had been focusing on 'who,' but Hyeolcheon Doma had focused on 'why.'

"Someone who wanted to build merit for Jin Hagun..."

"Or it means he definitely needed the head of Cheonhwa Sect."

At my words, Hyeolcheon Doma nodded.

"If so, he will certainly show some reaction because of this matter. You need only use that reaction as an opportunity to catch him."

"As expected! You are the greatest, Elder. How would I live without you?"

"Mask wearer, poisoner, drunkard, martial arts fanatic..."

Hyeolcheon Doma glanced at Gwonma sitting beside him.

I said with a smile.

"You should say fist fanatic."

"You and your master will live well."

Still, since Gwonma was sitting beside him, he showed him courtesy.

"If he weren't sitting beside you, he would have been a fist fanatic, right?"

Hyeolcheon Doma drank instead of answering, and Gwonma, who rarely smiled, formed a smile.

"I will send word to Tongcheon Pavilion as well, and instruct my intelligence organization to focus all information networks on this matter."

Gwonma, who had been listening to the conversation until now, finally opened his mouth.

"I shall see how they move before returning."

Gwonma seemed to have judged that there might be something he could help with.

"As expected! How would I live without my master?"

I had wanted to hear "bookworm" come from Gwonma's mouth, but he merely silently tilted his cup.

I also emptied my cup with them. I was curious how the opponent would make their move.

* * *

When Jin Hagun visited his master's house, Baek Cheongyeong was carving a wooden doll.

"I have returned, Master."

Baek Cheongyeong did not acknowledge the greeting and focused solely on carving the wood.

Scritch, scritch.

Jin Hagun watched his master's mood. He could not tell whether he still didn't know the news from Honam, or if he had already heard it. He had come to meet his master immediately after returning and paying respects to his grandfather.

His grandfather's reaction had been beyond expectations. When he told him that he could not forgive the atrocities of the Cheonhwa Sect successor and had ultimately given up, the expression on his grandfather's face had clearly been joy.

It was horrifying to imagine what emotion would have clouded that expression if he had returned having obtained Cheonhwa Sect. He felt grateful to Geommugeuk once more.

'I will surely repay you someday.'

The problem was his master. Because he had ultimately failed the task his master had proposed.

'Still, he will understand, won't he?'

Because the purpose of proposing this matter had also been to establish him as the successor.

Scritch, scritch.

While looking at his master's back, he suddenly recalled words Geommugeuk had spoken.

"Have you truly been looking straight at your grandfather until now?"

It hadn't been just his grandfather. He hadn't been looking straight at his master either.

Because he was his grandfather. Because he was his master.

Now he would look straight. He would look straight at the other without any prejudice.

Since his master was still focused on carving, Jin Hagun looked around the workshop as usual and went to the display stand where the dolls were placed.

A small doll had fallen over in front of the hero doll that had been leading the battle. It was a doll he hadn't seen when he left.

Just as Jin Hagun was setting up the fallen doll, his master spoke from behind.

"Leave it lying as it is."

Chapter 277: Can you handle it?
-------------------------------
Jinha-gun let go of the doll he had been trying to stand up.

Ssk-ssk.

The sound of carving wood was heard again.

Normally, his Master wouldn’t have cared whether a doll was standing or lying down.

Who was this doll, that he did?

Jinha-gun looked down at the fallen doll again.

For some reason, his eyes kept being drawn to it. In the end, though he didn’t dare stand it up, Jinha-gun quietly flipped over the doll that had been lying face-down.

In that moment, Jinha-gun was startled.

The wooden doll was him. It was finger-sized, but he could instinctively feel that it was definitely himself. The clothes and the hair were the same.

“Is it me?”

At the low question, the sound of wood carving stopped.

“Yes, it’s you.”

Jinha-gun looked at his Master with a puzzled heart.

‘If it’s me, why did you tell me to leave it lying down?’

Jinha-gun could tell. That his Master knew about what had happened in Honam.

‘Even so.’

Rather than this, he wished his Master would just get angry. He felt worse than if he had been scolded. What exactly had his Master been thinking while carving this doll, and while laying it down in front of him like this? He felt a sense of disappointment and distance that he had never felt before.

‘I thought you would understand why I did it.’

Yes, his Master was doing this because he didn’t know the circumstances of that day.

“The son of the Cheonhwamun Lord murdered innocent people.”

His Master remained silent, focused on the carving. Since he was listening, it meant to continue.

Jinha-gun couldn’t speak the most important part of that explanation. He should have said that he realized abandoning Cheonhwamun was his grandfather’s test, but then wouldn’t that mean his Master hadn’t realized it was his grandfather’s test from the beginning?

“Because the Sword Immortal intervened, I had no choice.”

Whether he already knew this, or whether he thought it was an excuse, his Master said nothing.

If this were the past, he would have been frantic, begging his Master for forgiveness first.

But Jinha-gun simply looked at his Master quietly.

‘What would Geom Mugik have done?’

Perhaps because he had been thinking of Geom Mugik the entire way back? That thought really did cross his mind, like a lie.

His appearance came to mind. His face, looking at him with a bright smile.

—Let’s have a drink!

One thing was certain. Geom Mugik would have smiled and handled even this moment.

Yes, if you get excited, you lose.

Jinha-gun spoke calmly.

“I’m sorry for disappointing you.”

At that moment, the sound of carving stopped.

Baek Cheon-gyeong slowly raised his head and looked at Jinha-gun.

If this were the past, his heart would have been beating as if it would burst, but strangely, he wasn’t afraid.

A strange light flickered in Baek Cheon-gyeong’s eyes. He sensed that his disciple was different from usual.

Baek Cheon-gyeong put down the doll he was holding and rose from his seat.

And slowly walked toward Jinha-gun.

Jinha-gun looked at his Master quietly. If this were the past, his head would have moved before his eyes.

The Master’s mood must be like this now. What should I say to make his anger subside?

But the current Jinha-gun was thinking differently. About those words of Geom Mugik that had taken root in his heart.

—Have you been looking properly all this time?

Jinha-gun looked at his Master walking toward him.

His Master’s gaze, footsteps, hand movements, and even expression.

‘Strange.’

Baek Cheon-gyeong’s gaze, which had been fixed on his disciple, turned toward the doll that had been collapsed on the display stand.

“Were you disappointed that your doll had fallen?”

Jinha-gun answered in a calm tone.

“Because it’s the first doll you made for me, Master.”

Baek Cheon-gyeong stood the doll back in its place.

“Is this better?”

Jinha-gun looked at the doll for a moment, then laid it down again.

“No, this time I lost to Geom Mugik. It should be lying down.”

As if he had been waiting for that reaction, Baek Cheon-gyeong nodded his head.

“Right. You should have somehow taken Cheonhwamun into your hands and saved them.”

Jinha-gun looked at Baek Cheon-gyeong silently. Indeed, it was strange. Now that he thought about it, he had never looked at his face so closely from the side, and from so near. Even when looking at his Master, it was just a glance; even when looking at his grandfather, it was just a glance. That’s how he had lived, looking at people.

“If a war breaks out someday, hundreds of righteous faction martial artists might die because of Cheonhwamun. Can you bear that responsibility?”

If it were the past, he would have immediately prostrated himself and begged his Master for forgiveness. Because he would have felt the weight of what his Master was saying now. No, even if he hadn’t felt it, he would have thought his Master was right.

But now, he didn’t feel that way. Instead, he felt this:

‘Why bring up an extreme example that hasn’t even happened?’

Suddenly, Geom Mugik’s words came to mind.

—The person who connected you with that Cheonhwamun Lord does not wish for you to become the Alliance Leader.

At the time he had thought it was nonsense, but now it had grown into a small doubt in his heart.

“My thoughts were short-sighted.”

At those words, Baek Cheon-gyeong’s expression relaxed slightly.

“Yes, as long as you know. You must find a way to reclaim Cheonhwamun even now.”

“I will think about it.”

Baek Cheon-gyeong returned to his seat. Before resuming his carving, he muttered a word.

“The Alliance Leader would have wanted that too.”

Ssk-ssk.

Until the end, it was at odds with Jinha-gun’s thoughts.

‘Did he really?’

His grandfather had sincerely rejoiced that he had abandoned Cheonhwamun. Because he had tried to look at his grandfather properly, he could read that emotion accurately. If not, he would have hung his head and agreed to those words. Because he hadn’t looked at his grandfather properly.

Only today did Jinha-gun realize how important it was to look properly, as Geom Mugik had said.

‘Leaving everything else aside, shouldn’t we not forgive someone like Seocheong? Because we are the righteous faction. Because among the righteous faction, I am the one who will become the Alliance Leader, and you are that Leader’s Master.’

After meeting Geom Mugik and returning, his Master looked different.

No, perhaps he was only realizing today that his Master might be an entirely different person from what he had perceived.

* * *

That night, Jinha-gun had a nightmare.

He was chased all night by a shadow-like being whose face he couldn’t recognize. No matter how much he ran, his opponent knew where he was and pursued him.

Even now, that thing was following him from far behind.

He was too tired to run.

Moving his steps heavily in a state of despair, people on the side of the road were looking at him.

Among them were faces he knew. There were those who had trained in martial arts with him as a child, and there were quarrels. People he hadn’t been particularly close with. Why did these people have to appear? Nightmares always summon those one has forgotten.

Passing by them, he continued walking. Meanwhile, the shadow chasing him drew closer and closer.

He thought he would die now. Then someone called out to him.

—Young Lord Jin!

When he raised his head, Geom Mugik was looking down at him, leaning on the railing of the second floor of a tavern.

—Come up and have a drink.

He was smiling brightly, just like at their last parting.

As if entranced, he went up to the second floor of the tavern. He was glad to see him.

Standing on the second-floor railing with Geom Mugik. The dark shadow that had been chasing him only looked up at him from the tavern entrance, unable to enter. He felt that the terrifying shadow was afraid.

Then Geom Mugik, standing beside him, suddenly spoke. It was what he had said to him at the banquet hall.

—Is that not why we exist? If not us, who could stop such a one?

In that moment as he looked at Geom Mugik, Jinha-gun woke from his dream.

“Whew.”

Soaked in sweat, Jinha-gun let out a sigh.

It wasn’t strange that Geom Mugik had appeared in his dream. Because he had been thinking of him constantly since meeting him.

But the choices he had made in the dream today were too foolish. He had just been chased helplessly. Whether fighting directly, leading the Demon Extermination Corps to fight, or going to his grandfather to ask for help. He should have made active efforts and choices. He had just been chased.

What made him angrier was that in the end, he had given up on everything. He remembered vividly that moment when he had been walking heavily in despair.

Shaking off the nightmare, he sprang up from his bed.

* * *

Jinha-ryeong was startled by Jinha-gun’s visit.

“Even my brother does things he doesn’t usually do?”

Because Jinha-gun hadn’t visited her quarters recently.

“People change.”

“Don’t change too much, it’s scary.”

Looking around the room, Jinha-gun sat down. Because her older brother wouldn’t have come to see her for no reason, Jinha-ryeong inwardly tensed and asked.

“How did it go?”

“I failed.”

“For a failure, you look well.”

“It was half failure and half success.”

Jinha-ryeong looked at him with an expression asking what he meant, but Jinha-gun didn’t answer. Though he had said that, he himself didn’t know. What was right and what was wrong now.

“How was Geom Mugik?”

“I don’t know.”

“Don’t be like that, tell me.”

“I said I don’t know. I don’t know that person.”

Jinha-ryeong nodded her head.

“You saw him well. That person.”

She too, if asked what kind of person Geom Mugik was, could only give that answer.

Then Jinha-gun brought up something unexpected.

“Ha-ryeong, have you ever experienced something in life you couldn’t understand?”

Jinha-ryeong looked at Jinha-gun with a surprised heart.

“Right now.”

Jinha-gun smiled faintly.

“I’m asking seriously.”

Had there been such a thing? Ah, there had.

“When I met Geom Mugik, back then I couldn’t understand anything at all.”

“How did you get past it?”

“I didn’t get past it. I was swept away by that person and it passed.”

Now Jinha-gun too could understand exactly what that meant.

Jinha-ryeong could tell. That a wave of change had crashed into her brother’s life. She didn’t ask if it was because of Geom Mugik. Because he wouldn’t answer that it was, even if only out of pride.

Then Jinha-gun asked about an unexpected matter.

“Are you still holding the junior talents’ gatherings?”

“I am.”

“Many must come.”

“Do they come to see me? They come to get in Grandfather’s good graces.”

Jinha-ryeong invited the righteous faction’s junior talents in rotation. It was closer to the Alliance managing the junior talents on an organizational level rather than a personal gathering. She was in charge of that.

“There’s someone I want to invite.”

“Who?”

Then from Jinha-gun’s mouth came an unexpected name.

“Someone we must look at without prejudice.”

Jinha-ryeong was startled. He was talking about Geom Mugik right now.

“Why him?”

Jinha-gun answered.

“I need his help. Invite him as your guest, and arrange for him to meet me under the pretense of coincidence.”

He couldn’t call him directly. The person who would become the Alliance Leader couldn’t ask the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader for help.

“Are you serious?”

Jinha-gun nodded his head. It was enough to surprise his sister like that. Even he himself thought this was a crazy move.

But he felt it instinctively. The alienation he had felt from his Master was continuously stimulating his crisis instinct. If he lived not knowing as he had until now, perhaps it would be fine, but the doubt that had sprouted in his heart was growing day by day.

It was not a time to delay, nor a time to nurse his pride. He needed someone’s help, and he needed an outsider who could look at this situation objectively, not an insider entangled in interests. He felt that he mustn’t be powerless as in the dream.

“Why on earth?”

“You don’t need to know.”

“I’ll ask again. Is this a situation where the Demon Extermination Corps Lord needs the Demonic Cult Young Cult Leader’s help?”

Jinha-gun answered.

“Let’s say it’s a situation where your older brother needs your friend’s help.”

Even thinking about it himself, it was absurd. But his instinct, even mobilizing dreams, was speaking urgently. That he needed him.

Jinha-ryeong hesitated. Though this was the first request her brother had made of her since she had come of age, she hesitated.

Geom Mugik was a good memory for her too. She wanted to see him again under this pretext.

But him getting entangled with her older brother was a different matter. It was a relationship between the successor to the Martial Arts Alliance and the successor to the Demonic Cult.

So she was uneasy. Whether it was alright to draw someone from the Demonic Cult into her brother’s affairs.

“Can you handle it?”

After a brief pause, Jinha-gun shook his head.

“Geom Mugik? I can’t. Because he’s someone I can’t handle, that’s why I need his help.”

Because he had accurately assessed the opponent, Jinha-ryeong felt relieved instead.

“Then that’s fine.”

* * *

I was training in the Sicheonbisul within the Sigong Ihwan Technique.

Since returning to the past, I had met all sorts of people and handled all sorts of matters, but I had never forgotten that martial arts training was the most important thing. Whenever I had time alone, I always devoted myself to training.

The Sicheonbisul was developing little by little. It was truly slow, like a tortoise. But I didn’t give up. I trained and trained again, hoping that one day my realm would suddenly rise dramatically. Of course, such a thing never happened.

When I had to be in places where people came and went, I would briefly close my eyes and train in the Cheonma Hoshinsul. Because I had left it one step before grand accomplishment, I trained with all my might.

For the Guhwa Demon Art, whenever I had spare moments, I only repeated the First Form. With the Flying Heaven Sword Art that had achieved the Grand Perfection of the Twelve Stars, there would be almost no opponent I couldn’t kill, but if one appeared, I planned to kill them with the First Form. So I trained in repetition, again and again. I had seen the Four Evil Ghosts so much that I now felt a sense of intimacy with them. The time they stayed was also subtly increasing.

Having finished training like that and resting for a moment, I saw Gwonma taking a walk outside the window.

Gwonma stood tall at the end of the courtyard, looking beyond the wall at the distant mountain.

“Master.”

I stood side by side next to Gwonma.

“Shall I guess what you were just thinking?”

At that, Gwonma showed interest.

“What was I thinking?”

“You were thinking of the cliff, right?”

“If someone heard that, they’d misunderstand me as being crazy about breaking cliffs. Why did you think that?”

“Because your back told me as much, Master.”

Gwonma looked at me with an expression asking what I meant.

There are people who tell you who they are with their backs. My father is like that, and Gwonma is like that.

The reason loneliness is felt when looking at his back is probably because one knows how stubborn that back is. Because it is a back that would walk alone toward thousands or tens of thousands of enemies without a moment’s hesitation if it were Father’s command.

“Master, I don’t think you should break the cliff.”

“Why?”

“When will you clean all that up? And it’s only cool because that cliff is there, isn’t it?”

Gwonma looked at me and smiled. This man’s smile, this smile that is not seen often, I like it.

Then a martial artist from the Honam branch came and delivered a message. Surprisingly, it was a message from Jinha-ryeong.

—Didn’t I say that if something strange and incomprehensible happens in life, you must absolutely tell me? That time is right now.

Chapter 278: Nervous Seeing Hubei's Number One Beauty After So Long?
--------------------------------------------------------------------
When I went with the Fist Demon to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s quarters, he was reading a book.

“What book are you reading after coming all the way here?”

Without even lifting his head, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon replied.

“You won’t send me home, so I have to read here at least.”

“Where did you get that book?”

“There was a library prepared for the branch’s martial artists.”

As if martial artists would read books. Yet he had gone through even that place, set up merely as a formality, and had somehow found something to read. In that respect, he truly was someone who loved books.

“Stop reading and look at this first.”

Only then did the Blood Heaven Blade Demon close his book and raise his head.

“A letter has come from the Martial Alliance Leader’s granddaughter.”

“Why would the Martial Alliance Leader’s granddaughter write?”

“She sent an invitation to a gathering of rising young talents, along with a request for help. It is definitely related to this matter.”

“So the other side has begun to react.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had advised that we should respond according to their reaction, and now word had come from someone unexpected.

“My guess is that the person who connected the Cheonhwa Sect Leader and Jin Hagun is pressuring Jin Hagun over the failure of this matter.”

For Jin Haryeong to ask to see me at a time like this likely meant that some problem had arisen for her elder brother, Jin Hagun.

This time, the Fist Demon asked.

“What will you do?”

“A friend has asked for help, so I should go.”

The Fist Demon’s expression grew serious.

“You must be careful about going to the Martial Alliance.”

My contacting them in the past and contacting them now, after becoming Young Cult Leader, were entirely different matters. My actions could become the official stance of our cult.

“I will move with caution.”

At that, the Fist Demon spoke firmly.

“We will go with you.”

Seeing as he said it without asking the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s opinion, it seemed they had already agreed to move together with me until this matter was over.

“If the two of you attend, the entire Martial Alliance will go on emergency alert. You are people who can put even our cult’s Hunan Branch on emergency alert.”

The two Demon Supremes smiled faintly. Even to them, the incident where the Hunan Branch had been thrown into a state of alarm must have seemed amusing.

If it had been the Poison King or the Drunken Demon, they might have been able to attend the banquet with me, but these two had to move without drawing attention.

“Please stay at the safe house closest to the Martial Alliance. If I need help, I will send word immediately.”

Just as the two Demon Supremes nodded, Jeogyeon came and reported that someone had arrived. Following an unexpected letter, it was the visit of an unexpected person. Coincidentally, both were women.

“Martial Artist Yi has arrived.”

* * *

Yi An was entering the Hunan Branch. Wearing a mask, she was dressed in martial attire different from her usual clothes, and every martial artist in the branch stopped in their tracks and stared at her.

Her beauty, which could not be hidden by the mask, was one thing, but the aura she exuded was far from ordinary. From her stride alone, one could feel the momentum of an expert who overwhelmed common martial artists.

She wore black martial robes and a black mask, and on the mask, the words Ghost Shadow were embroidered in white.

Behind her, Blue Mask entered with over a dozen martial artists. They were all dressed the same, and each one of them possessed an extraordinary presence.

“Yi An!”

At my call, Yi An looked this way. Normally, she would have come running to me after not seeing me for so long, shouting, Young Master! But conscious of the eyes around us, she walked slowly toward me.

—You had new martial clothes made? They look great!

At my voice transmission, Yi An quickly replied.

—Don’t talk to me! I’m going to greet you impressively.

—Right now, you’re moving the arm and leg on the same side.

—Don’t! Don’t tease me! This is my first greeting to Young Master, so I’m going to do it properly.

When she reached me, she cupped her fist respectfully and greeted me.

“I greet the Young Cult Leader.”

—So fake!

—Bear with it!

The martial artists who had come with her lined up behind her.

“We greet the Young Cult Leader.”

At their resounding greeting, Yi An introduced them.

“They’re the First Team of the Ghost Shadow Unit.”

Then Blue Mask stepped forward and greeted me.

“I am Blue Mask, leader of the First Team. I formally greet the Young Cult Leader.”

At last, the First Team of the Ghost Shadow Unit was complete. Blue Mask was still wearing his blue mask, and the eyes behind it looked more reliable than ever. He was a man who had cast aside a place as a future Demon Supreme and chosen to be a team leader. I believed the First Team he led would surpass the first team of any other organization in murim.

“For now, there is only the First Team, but we will gradually expand.”

I could feel pride from Yi An. Blue Mask added his loyalty to it.

“We carry out only the Young Cult Leader’s orders.”

“Any order?”

“Yes, that is correct.”

“What would you do if I ordered you to go kill the Martial Alliance Leader right now?”

Though it was a suicide mission, Blue Mask answered without hesitation.

“We would carry out the order immediately.”

“What if my father gives an order? And what if that order conflicts with mine?”

For a moment, Blue Mask was flustered, but he soon answered calmly.

“We serve only the Young Cult Leader’s orders.”

“I am grateful for your loyalty to me, but my father’s orders are an exception. Regard my father’s orders as my own and follow them. Understood?”

“Yes, understood!”

With deep trust, I patted Blue Mask on the shoulder.

“I will be counting on you from now on.”

“I will serve you with all my loyalty.”

The real request, however, I made through voice transmission.

—Rather than me, take good care of your unit commander.

Blue Mask answered with the look in his eyes behind the mask. It was a gaze more trustworthy than any words.

Passing Blue Mask, I slowly walked before the First Team members. They were all young. Since Yi An and Blue Mask had carefully selected them, they were martial artists I could trust.

As I passed, each of them removed their mask and stated their name.

After meeting each of their eyes, I spoke to them.

“There is something I always say to your commanders. Cherish yourselves most of all. Live for your own happiness.”

They all looked as if they had heard this before. Yi An had probably told them about me.

I asked one of the Ghost Shadow Unit’s martial artists.

“Do you know why it was named the Ghost Shadow Unit?”

“I heard it was named from the resolve to become the Young Cult Leader’s shadow.”

“Yes, that is correct. You are my shadows.”

At that moment, I sensed Jeogyeon, who stood behind me, tense and waver slightly. The aura of the Ghost Shadow Unit’s martial artists was no less than that of the guards, and seeing them named my shadows seemed to have stirred a strange sense of rivalry. She must have considered the shadows to be themselves.

Pretending not to notice, I continued speaking to the Ghost Shadow Unit.

“If the main body is happy but the shadow is unhappy, wouldn’t that be strange? So don’t make me into a bad person. Let the shadow become happier than the main body. I am someone who believes this organization will become stronger only when you are happy. Now, repeat after me. We will become happy shadows!”

“We will become happy shadows.”

Even as they repeated it, the First Team members looked awkward. They may have heard that I was an unusual Young Cult Leader, but they surely had not expected to be made to recite such a shout.

Happiness.

It was probably a word that had never been emphasized in any organization of the Demon Cult until now. No, it likely had not been emphasized in the orthodox or unorthodox factions either.

Then Yi An stepped in and spoke to them.

“You do understand that those words do not mean you should become cowards, right?”

“Of course!”

A resounding answer rang out.

“You worked hard coming here. Jeogyeon, provide the Ghost Shadow Unit with food and rooms. And Unit Leader Yi, come see me for a moment.”

After letting the Ghost Shadow Unit rest, Yi An and I walked through the garden together.

“How did you come here?”

“I heard you were here, Young Master, so I stopped by on my way back to the cult. It was on the way.”

“You didn’t come to show off your First Team members?”

“Was it obvious?”

She smiled awkwardly. She had come running to me at once because she wanted to show them off.

“I wanted to show them to you first, Young Master. What do you think of my subordinates?”

I held back my answer for a moment. As I was wondering how to tease her when she was so tense, she beat me to it.

“I’m relieved. Thank you for saying I picked them well.”

“I didn’t say anything.”

“I can tell just from seeing you think about how to tease me. If I had messed up, you wouldn’t even be thinking about teasing me.”

At quick-witted Yi An’s words, I offered sincere congratulations instead of a joke.

“Yes, they all looked good. You worked hard choosing them.”

Yi An smiled brightly. With that single sentence, she must have felt her past hardships rewarded.

“How was it for you?”

“While traveling around the Central Plains this time, I realized again and again that I had only been living inside a well. I also felt how important experience truly is.”

“In that sense, come with me to gain one more experience. Since an invitation arrived on the very day you got here, it must be fate telling us to go together.”

On the day Jin Haryeong’s letter arrived, Yi An arrived as well. I took it as fate telling me to let the two women meet. Since both of them were extraordinarily intelligent women, I believed this meeting would help them grow in some way.

There was also one more reason.

I recalled the conversation I had had with Jin Haryeong.

—Do you perhaps have a woman back home? A woman you like?

—There is a woman prettier than you.

—Prettier than me? Then your credibility drops sharply.

—You are Hubei’s foremost beauty, aren’t you? That one is the greatest beauty under heaven.

—Next time, you must show me that greatest beauty under heaven!

I had meant to introduce her if the chance came, and now at last the chance had arrived.

“Who sent the invitation?”

I smiled faintly and said,

“There is someone. The person who made my credibility plummet.”

* * *

Jin Haryeong held the gathering as usual.

To take part in this gathering, the rising talents of the younger generation each made every possible effort. Only those who distinguished themselves in their respective regions could receive an invitation.

The gathering’s greatest advantage was that it provided an opportunity to build connections with the children of other sects. In particular, the gathering was meaningful simply because one could make an impression on Jin Haryeong.

The young talents attended the gathering while secretly dreaming of marriage to Jin Haryeong. Most of them were the most successful figures in their own towns, so they dared to dream big.

As she was the Martial Alliance Leader’s granddaughter, Hubei’s foremost beauty, and also outstanding in martial arts, she could be called the finest marriage prospect in the orthodox murim.

Of course, once they met Jin Haryeong in person, they realized that it was an unattainable dream. She led the gathering itself splendidly, but she never revealed any personal feelings. Especially after the last Soryongjeon incident, she had allowed not even the slightest opening.

“Is something the matter? You seem nervous.”

Jin Haryeong’s guard, Chuho, asked with some concern. Today, she looked especially tense.

“I’m fine.”

She did not assume Geom Mugeuk would naturally come. Unlike the last time they met, there was now a clear difference in their status. Simply coming to the Martial Alliance meant taking a great risk, so it was more likely that he would not come.

Even so, she found herself hoping for no reason. If it was Geom Mugeuk, she felt he would somehow come. He was a man who gave her that sort of expectation.

In the meantime, people began to gather in the banquet hall one after another. They exchanged greetings with one another, each striving to make themselves known.

She looked outside from behind the curtain, but Geom Mugeuk was nowhere to be seen.

‘Is he not coming?’

Putting aside disappointment or wounded pride, her elder brother was in a situation where he desperately needed him.

“It is time for you to go out.”

At Chuho’s words, she composed herself. The expression on her face as she went outside was completely different from before.

When she appeared, everyone applauded and cheered. Those seeing her for the first time were greatly amazed by the beauty of Hubei’s foremost beauty.

Normally, it would be customary to begin by thanking all the fellow martial artists who had honored the gathering with their presence.

“Good to see you, friends.”

When she opened with a joke, laughter burst out from all sides, and the atmosphere immediately improved.

“When my grandfather begins a speech, no matter how bored you are, none of you can even blink, right? Now then, it’s all right to doze off here.”

Laughter followed again. Originally, Jin Haryeong had not been this open and easygoing. But after meeting Geom Mugeuk, she had changed. She was trying to change. She herself did not know why.

“You have probably heard some unsettling news lately. News from the Demon Cult, and news from the Evil Dao Alliance.”

At the mention of the Demon Cult and the Evil Dao Alliance, everyone’s expressions stiffened. Most of those gathered were children of prestigious sects, so they had heard rumors about the successors of the Demon Cult and the Evil Dao Alliance through various channels. In particular, the rumors about the Demon Cult’s successor were so astonishing as to be almost unbelievable.

“That rumor tells us why we must gather, and why we must stand firmly united. Because someone more extraordinary than any successor in history has appeared. And the ones who will face him are not our grandfathers or our parents. In the future, it will be us.”

A heavy silence flowed through the hall.

Pausing her speech for a moment, Jin Ha-ryeong silently looked around at them. The young martial artists had to awaken. If they were to face him, they had to change in earnest.

It was at that very moment. Jin Ha-ryeong’s eyes met someone’s among the rising talents.

The instant she saw him, she was startled. When on earth had he come in? Amid the crowd stood Geom Mugeuk, smiling.

‘Ah! He came!’

A surge of joy welled up in her. Those clear, deep eyes, yet pure as if he might start playing a prank at any moment, were the same now as they had been then.

She was grateful to him. When she asked for help, he had not refused and had come running like this. Though he was the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult, he stood there alone among the people of the righteous faction. All because she had asked him to come.

Gazing at Geom Mugeuk, she continued her speech.

“The young cult leader of the Demonic Cult in the rumors is no ordinary person. They say he is stronger, smarter, and braver than anyone. They even say he keeps his promises well, don’t they?”

Geom Mugeuk’s smile deepened.

“So we, too, must stay alert and hone our skills. If we let our guard down, someday the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult’s sword will pierce our hearts. So let us all join forces and strive together! For the peace of the murim! There’s no time to be dozing off.”

The rising talents cheered with fervor.

In the noisy hall, the two of them simply gazed at each other.

* * *

After finishing her speech, Jin Ha-ryeong immediately met Geom Mugeuk separately in the rear garden.

“You really came.”

“We agreed to be friends, didn’t we?”

“It’s been so long that it feels awkward.”

“You can treat me comfortably. I came as a friend.”

Seeing Geom Mugeuk up close, Jin Ha-ryeong felt needlessly nervous and unsettled. With the matter involving her older brother as well, all sorts of thoughts ran through her mind. He really had come as a friend, hadn’t he? Could she perhaps be being deceived by this man? Gratitude and fear mingled together.

So she put on an unnecessary show of banter to ease her tension.

“Well? Seeing Hubei’s greatest beauty after so long, are you trembling?”

“I’m someone who sees the greatest beauty under heaven every day.”

“There you go boasting again.”

“I knew you’d say that, so I brought her with me.”

“Then show me this greatest beauty under heaven.”

Until that moment, Jin Ha-ryeong had thought Geom Mugeuk was joking.

But when Geom Mugeuk turned his head, Ian appeared from behind a building in the distance. Realizing he had truly come with a woman, Jin Ha-ryeong was startled.

Ian slowly emerged from the building’s shadow and began walking toward the two of them.

Chapter 279: You Never Know What Will Happen.
---------------------------------------------
Ian was wearing a bamboo hat with a veil hanging from it.

As she drew nearer and nearer, Jin Haryeong’s eyes grew wider and wider. She had a slender yet sensual figure, one truly not easily seen.

Even so, Haryeong still had some composure left.

Ian walked up before the two of them and slowly removed her bamboo hat.

The moment she saw Ian’s face, Jin Haryeong was shocked.

Haryeong had confidence in her looks. No matter how beautiful a woman might be, once she stood before Haryeong, she was bound to seem ordinary.

But this opponent was different. Her features were clear and refreshing, her eyes bright, and light seemed to shine from her supple skin.

She was so alluringly beautiful that one could scarcely believe it was allowed.

The reason Haryeong was more astonished and admiring than ordinary people would have been was because, when it came to beauty, Jin Haryeong herself understood it better than anyone.

She had been compared to others countless times, and she had met many beautiful women. That was why she knew. It was not easy to be that beautiful.

And there was one thing more.

There was something that made Ian perfect. It was Ian’s aura. Her aura was gentle, yet intense. Looking at her, Haryeong felt as though she were gazing upon a single plum blossom blooming on a snowy day.

‘She’s an expert!’

No young rising talent she had ever seen had possessed an aura like this. It was an aura that overwhelmed not only them, but even Haryeong herself.

‘She’s stronger than I am.’

She was this beautiful, and even her martial arts were powerful? Surely her personality couldn’t be good too, could it? Her voice must be strange, right? Haryeong found herself thinking such utterly pointless thoughts.

While she was lost in thought, Ian smiled and greeted her.

“It’s a pleasure to meet you. I’m Ian.”

Jade beads rolled across a tray. Since her personality was still unknown, judging only by outward elements for now, she was perfect.

“I’m Jin Haryeong.”

Jin Haryeong’s voice trembled slightly. She had not trembled even while giving a speech before all those young rising talents earlier, yet in this moment, she did.

Ian, on the other hand, was facing the granddaughter of the Murim Alliance Leader, yet every word she spoke was brimming with confidence.

“I’ve heard a great deal about you. It’s an honor to meet someone so esteemed. You’re truly beautiful. This is my first time meeting someone so beautiful.”

One phrase surfaced in Jin Haryeong’s mind.

‘Look who’s talking!’

To think the day would come when she would be outshone in looks.

Her startled gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk. Geom Mugeuk looked so smugly proud that it was annoying.

“This is the kind of man I am.”

Jin Haryeong confirmed it again, as if she could not believe it.

“She’s really your girlfriend?”

At the word girlfriend, this time Ian looked at Geom Mugeuk in surprise.

“She looked down on me for not having a girlfriend, so I told her I did.”

Jin Haryeong’s heart pounded in her own way, and Ian’s in hers.

“So is she your girlfriend or not?”

Just as Geom Mugeuk was about to answer, Ian replied.

“No.”

At that very moment, Jin Haryeong felt an inexplicable sense of relief.

“We’re much closer than girlfriend and boyfriend.”

Jin Haryeong flinched in surprise.

It was a brief reaction, but Ian was able to sense it.

‘Lady Jin likes the young master.’

It was an intuition she could feel because they were both women.

The Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult and the granddaughter of the Murim Alliance Leader.

‘The condition every man in the world would desire has, at least in a relationship with the young master, become the worst possible condition.’

Ian smiled and said,

“I’ve served him as his guard since we were young. I only said that because I’ve known him for a very long time.”

At that, Jin Haryeong glanced at Geom Mugeuk and said,

“It must have been difficult, serving that man.”

“It wasn’t easy.”

When the two women looked at him at the same time, Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“People naturally become close faster when they have a common enemy.”

Then Jin Haryeong asked provocatively,

“Is he really a common enemy?”

“What do you mean?”

Jin Haryeong said to Ian,

“If he saw you as a subordinate, I don’t think the word girlfriend would ever have come out of that man’s mouth.”

At those words, spoken as if probing for no particular reason, Ian replied firmly.

“You misunderstand. To me, he is the esteemed Young Cult Leader, and I have never dared harbor such feelings.”

Her feelings of liking Geom Mugeuk were deeper than anyone’s. But she was a mere guard, while Geom Mugeuk was of the Heavenly Demon’s bloodline. She herself was even completely alone in the world, without knowing who her parents were.

“You never know what will happen in the world.”

Jin Haryeong’s words were directed at Ian, and perhaps also at herself.

Just as Ian had sensed Jin Haryeong’s fondness for Geom Mugeuk, Jin Haryeong also sensed that Ian liked Geom Mugeuk.

For a very brief moment, a strange current flowed between the two women.

Jin Haryeong said to Geom Mugeuk,

“Anyway, I’ll take back calling you a braggart.”

“Then that’s settled. My credibility has been restored.”

Soon after, Geom Mugeuk revealed the reason he had brought Ian.

“I didn’t bring Ian here to make the two of you compete over beauty.”

“Then why?”

The reason was entirely unexpected.

“I brought my friend to introduce her to another friend. I thought the two of you would get along well.”

The words “another friend” flew into Jin Haryeong’s heart.

‘So he really did think of me as a friend.’

It was a strange feeling, pleasant yet a little disappointing.

After introducing the two women to each other like that, Geom Mugeuk got to the point.

“Where is Unit Leader Jin?”

“Why do you ask about my brother?”

“Wasn’t he the reason you called me?”

“How did you know?”

Really, this man no longer even surprised her.

“Your brother must have asked you to arrange a chance meeting with me.”

“How did you know that too? Don’t tell me. Are you watching us? Did you plant people around me?”

Because it was sincere and not a joke, the words instead made Geom Mugeuk laugh.

“I truly wish I had spies that capable. The problem is, people that skilled don’t become spies.”

That meant Geom Mugeuk had figured it out through deduction. Yes, it was because he was such a person that her brother had asked for his help.

“Do you think of me as a friend?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“Then promise me one thing. Don’t hurt my brother. If you’re going to do that, kill me instead. Understood?”

Geom Mugeuk said to Ian,

“Did you hear that? If I give an order to kill Unit Leader Jin, then you should kill this person here.”

“You introduced us so we could be friends, and yet isn’t that a rather cruel order?”

At Ian’s words, Jin Haryeong smiled faintly.

“My brother is in the guest room at the end of the rear garden over there. If I send word, he’s waiting to come out and meet you.”

“I’ll go meet him.”

“My brother’s pride will be wounded.”

“If he wanted to protect his pride, he wouldn’t have called me in the first place. Then I’ll see you later.”

After saying that, Geom Mugeuk walked toward the place Jin Haryeong had told him about.

When only the two of them remained, an awkward silence flowed between the women for a moment.

Jin Haryeong asked Ian,

“Would you like to go to the banquet together?”

If the two beautiful women returned to the banquet hall together, it would truly cause an uproar.

Ian politely declined.

“Please invite me next time. Today, I don’t think it’s a place I should attend.”

“Yes, then I’ll see you next time.”

They exchanged polite greetings with fist salutes.

After walking a few steps, Jin Haryeong looked back at Ian.

“Will we be able to meet again?”

Jin Haryeong felt curious about Ian. She wondered what kind of woman she was, and she was also curious how the Geom Mugeuk Ian knew differed from the Geom Mugeuk she herself knew. The fact that Ian was beautiful also drew her in even more.

“Of course. I will always be with the young master. If you wish to see me, please seek out the young master.”

“I will.”

Jin Haryeong returned to the banquet hall, and Ian quietly disappeared into the shadows.

* * *

Jin Hagun was waiting in the guest room.

When his sister contacted him, he intended to go there naturally and meet Geom Mugeuk. It would not be strange for him to participate in a gathering of young rising talents.

‘This is madness.’
