“Step aside. I cannot stand by and watch someone dare impersonate the Young Cult Leader so insolently.”

“I cannot allow that.”

The two men glared at each other. It was the moment the rift they had hidden until now was laid bare in all its depth. In truth, Jungak had expected Hogyeong to block him.

—Kill that bastard!

A sound transmission flew to Chahoe, his trusted limb. This was the perfect opportunity, while Hogyeong was blocking him.

Chahoe launched a surprise sword qi strike.

Shiiiing!

A rough streak of sword qi flew toward Juyang. It was a powerful sword qi that swept toward even Geom Mugeuk, who was standing with him.

Boom!

Startled by the explosive sound, Juyang fell backward onto his rear.

He saw Geom Mugeuk’s back. Geom Mugeuk had remained where he stood and lightly dispelled the incoming sword qi with a swing of his sword.

“Attack together and kill him!”

At the order of First Squad Leader Chahoe, the demonic practitioners of the Demonic Sword Unit drew their swords all at once.

At the same time, Hogyeong shouted as well.

“Stop them!”

The demonic practitioners belonging to the branch also drew their swords in unison. A taut tension settled between the two groups as they aimed their blades at one another. If they clashed like this, the branch warriors would be no match for the elite Demonic Sword Unit.

It was at that very moment.

Geom Mugeuk walked forward and released his aura.

Swaaaaaaaaaa!

Geom Mugeuk’s aura spread in every direction.

A massive wave came crashing toward them.

There was nowhere to avoid it, and they could not avoid it.

All the demonic practitioners sank into the water. The abyss dragged them endlessly downward.

They resisted. They struggled with all their might not to be pulled in. But Geom Mugeuk’s aura allowed no resistance.

Their entire bodies felt as if they would burst, and their breath was choked off.

‘Uaaaaaaaah!’

They screamed, but no voice came out.

And at the end of that abyss, they saw it.

The figure of Geom Mugeuk, standing alone and shining within the pitch-black darkness.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk’s aura vanished.

The Geom Mugeuk of the abyss became the Geom Mugeuk of reality, standing before them.

Everyone exhaled with difficulty, aghast. Even without drawing swords, they could tell. Geom Mugeuk’s martial arts were overwhelmingly beyond comparison with theirs. They also understood that what had been constricting them was demonic energy.

Geom Mugeuk said to Hogyeong,

“It has been a long time. At the celebration for your appointment as Branch Master, you greeted everyone like this, did you not? That you would carefully examine the small things and prevent great calamities.”

Hogyeong remembered it vividly. It was the greeting he had given at the celebration after receiving his first appointment as Branch Master. How could he ever forget that day?

“You were there?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“Who are you, young hero?”

At that moment, someone appeared there.

When Hogyeong saw the newcomer, he was startled.

“Branch Master Lin!”

He was Lin Qing, the Jiangxi Branch Master of Tongtian Pavilion.

A man who did not show himself unless it was important had appeared here.

“What brings you here?”

Before coming here, Geom Mugeuk had met a warrior of Tongtian Pavilion and, through him, had asked Lin Qing to handle this matter.

“I have come to convey an order that every warrior belonging to the Jiangxi Branch is to serve the Young Cult Leader with utmost sincerity.”

At the mention of the Young Cult Leader, everyone was shocked. Had they not been mocking the fake Young Cult Leader just moments ago?

“Surely not!”

Everyone’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk, and Lin Qing spoke loudly.

“That is correct. This person is the Young Cult Leader.”

Silence fell in shock and astonishment.

The first to come to his senses was Hogyeong. He observed proper etiquette and prostrated himself.

“I greet the Young Cult Leader!”

At that, not only the demonic practitioners of the branch, but Jungak and the Demonic Sword Unit as well all paid their respects at once. Under any circumstances, reverence toward the Cult Leader and the Young Cult Leader was absolute.

“We greet the Young Cult Leader!”

The voices of the demonic practitioners rang out thunderously.

In truth, even if Lin Qing had not confirmed it, the grand aura he had already shown was enough to know that he was the Young Cult Leader.

Now the only person standing there blankly was Juyang.

Geom Mugeuk looked at him and smiled faintly.

“You believe me now, even without the Demon Venerables standing behind me, don’t you?”

Juyang, belatedly coming to his senses, shrieked and threw himself flat on the ground.

“I failed to recognize your noble self and committed a discourtesy.”

“Discourtesy? You were the only one who believed I was the Young Cult Leader.”

Juyang was overwhelmed with emotion. He could not believe this was real. Then did that mean he had met the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult all this time, received interest from him, and conversed with him? He had even called him a madman.

“You will have to suffer a little from now on.”

Only now did he understand. The source of that absurd confidence.

How could he possibly neglect the matter of Tongjeonso when it was an order from the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult?

“I will work for the rest of my life with a heart of atonement.”

What more could he ask for when his life had been saved? Until just a moment ago, he had been floundering in the sea, but now he had begun floating comfortably again. Warm sunlight poured down on him.

By contrast, a great wave was raging in Jungak’s heart.

‘Why in the world is the Young Cult Leader here?’

The one targeting him had been Hogyeong, had it not? Could Hogyeong have joined hands with him to target him? But judging from Hogyeong’s startled reaction, it was clear he too had not expected the Young Cult Leader’s appearance.

“Now, everyone rise!”

Every demonic practitioner there rose from their place. They still gazed at Geom Mugeuk with eyes full of shock and awe. He was the man who would become Cult Leader. If he commanded, “Die!” they would have to die on the spot.

Geom Mugeuk walked toward Jungak.

“Unit Leader Jung, you accepted quite a lot of bribes.”

Before Jungak could feign ignorance, Lin Qing spoke first.

“It has been confirmed that gold from Tongjeonso was divided and stored in several money houses under Unit Leader Jung’s name. The investigation is ongoing, so we expect other evidence to emerge soon as well. In addition, investigations have begun into places other than Tongjeonso.”

If someone else had said it, he could have insisted it was fabricated, but these were the words of a Branch Master of Tongtian Pavilion.

Even so, Jungak held his head high and spoke as if it were nothing.

“I did not extort it by force. They offered it voluntarily.”

“Would they have given you money even if you were not the Leader of the Demonic Sword Unit?”

“It was their choice.”

Because his subordinates were watching, he stood firm. The moment he showed servility, his subordinates’ respect would vanish.

“Did you share that money with your men?”

A silence flowed through the air. The only person he had shared money with was Chahoe, his trusted limb.

“I intended to share it.”

He would have been better off not saying that. At that shabby excuse, the gazes of the Demonic Sword Unit members turned cold.

“I believe the authority of the Demonic Sword Unit should not be damaged over such a trivial matter.”

Jungak was trying by any means to get through this moment. But his opponent was Geom Mugeuk.

“That is exactly why.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at the demonic practitioners of the Demonic Sword Unit.

“Because you are the Demonic Sword Unit. Even if the Martial Alliance or the Evil Dao Alliance pushed us all the way back to the main headquarters, if we heard that you were still alive, we would not lose hope. We would believe that you would break through the enemies and come to save us. That is the faith our cult places in you, the Demonic Sword Unit.”

Pride bloomed on the faces of the Demonic Sword Unit’s demonic practitioners.

“That is why you must be different from others. You cannot simply take bribes because they are offered. You might think, ‘This much should be fine, shouldn’t it?’ It is not fine. If you are the kind of people who take bribes and think nothing of it, then we will not wait for the Demonic Sword Unit. Because we will think you compromised with something easier instead of choosing to break through the enemy. You will not come, and we will not wait.”

Jungak’s expression twisted, but the demonic practitioners of the Demonic Sword Unit were sympathizing with Geom Mugeuk’s words.

Jungak shouted as if protesting.

“Why are you worrying in advance about things that have not even happened?”

“Because I am the one who will become your Cult Leader. Even if no one else does, I must worry about that.”

Jungak could not refute him.

And another crime was added on top.

“The reason I cannot forgive you is not the bribes. Today, here, you hoped Branch Master Ho would die or be injured.”

At those words, a stir ran through the surroundings.

“That is not true. If that were the case, why would I have come here?”

“Because the task of finding the one who harmed the Branch Master would fall to you anyway. You likely intended to finish everything here. Kill Branch Master Ho and take revenge as well.”

“Why would I have done that?”

The one who answered was Hogyeong.

“Because you found out I had been investigating you.”

Only now could Hogyeong be certain.

“That was why you came to me and guided me into personally taking on the mission.”

Jungak was about to shout that they should not frame him, but then his eyes met those of his subordinates. In those eyes, which had once feared him, there was now distrust and disappointment. Their respect was disappearing.

“If you lose me, it will be a great blow to our cult’s fighting strength.”

“If we consider the possibility that your sword might be pointed at us because of money, it might actually be a gain.”

“That would never have happened.”

“Who knows? What might happen if you were offered too much money to refuse?”

To Jungak, whose face was full of resentment, Geom Mugeuk spoke calmly.

“That is why. That is why you should not have accepted it from the beginning. Whether your words are true or not, everything ends up a mess like this. You become someone who looks as if he would sell me out for a few coins.”

Geom Mugeuk spoke sincerely.

“Pay for your sins and start again. When that time comes, I will give you another chance.”

For the first time, Jungak let out a long sigh.

How many years would he have to live in the dungeon? Five years? Ten?

“I do not wish to do that.”

What Jungak chose was to kill Hogyeong and die himself. He needed someone to resent, and the target of that resentment was not himself.

‘This is all your fault!’

He hurled himself at him and drew his sword.

Flash!

Just as his swift blade was about to cleave through Hogyeong’s body.

His sword, which could slice through even a massive boulder, was blocked by something—and snapped.

Clang!

The instant Jungak confirmed his opponent, his eyes bulged wide.

Pwaaaagh!

The one who had broken his sword and cut open his chest again was a terrifying evil spirit.

Slice! Slice! Slice!

From the left, the right, and behind him, the swords of evil spirits came flying in. As his entire body was carved apart, Jungak breathed his last.

Those watching were so shocked they could barely breathe.

Knowing that the Young Cult Leader’s demonic art that summoned evil spirits could only be the Guhwa Demonic Art, they all bowed their heads at once and showed their respect. This time, it was not courtesy toward Geom Mugeuk, but toward the Guhwa Demonic Art.

Seizing that moment, Chahoe, who had been Jungak’s hands and feet, tried to fulfill his dead leader’s will, but he was cut down by Hogyeong’s sword and collapsed lifelessly.

The warriors of the branch were astonished by Hogyeong’s skill. With his meticulous nature, they had only ever seen him sitting at a desk, shuffling through documents, yet Hogyeong was far more of an expert than they had imagined. Because he had never once shown off his abilities, their admiration was all the greater. If they had known this, they would have grumbled less about the increased workload.

Unlike the branch warriors, whose morale had risen, only gloom hung over the Demon Sword Squad. Corrupt warriors though they had been, they had lost their commander and their First Unit Leader.

Geom Mugeuk soothed their hearts.

“By now, at the tavern in Maga Village I often visit, everyone will be drinking. Worries about money, worries about children, worries about work, worries about people. They’ll have all kinds of worries, but at least they won’t worry that warriors of the Sado Alliance will open the tavern door, walk in, and kill them. Why? Because you are protecting this Jiangxi Branch.”

Geom Mugeuk spoke with sincerity and honesty.

“I know we give you the best treatment, and I know even that is not enough. With your skills, you could go out and earn ten times more. But in return, you would not gain the honor of being the elite Demon Sword Squad of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. You may ask whether honor puts food on the table, but you must live on honor. Because you are the people who, with that honor, let others eat their meals in peace.”

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk added one last thing.

“Even if no one else recognizes it, I will.”

Pride and resolve entered the eyes of the Demon Sword Squad warriors. So what if it was merely comfort in words? It was comfort given by none other than the man who would become the Cult Leader.

“I will devote this one life to loyalty.”

The comfort for them was not over. It had only just begun.

“Now, everyone follow me.”

Both the Demon Sword Squad and the branch warriors followed behind Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk also spoke to Juyang.

“Lead the way.”

“Me?”

“This is where you will be the protagonist, so you should go.”

Juyang understood. He intended to take all of them to Jeokpa’s manor. It was not because he held Jeokpa in high regard.

Because Geom Mugeuk had said this to Im Jeong of the Tongcheon Pavilion.

“Send word to the Sado Alliance. Tell them it is the Jiangxi Branch’s day of unity, so they are not to bother us for today.”

Chapter 300: Today, Forget All Worldly Affairs.
-----------------------------------------------
Have you ever had an experience like this while living in the martial world?

The Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader was right beside him, the Demonic Cult’s Branch Altar Lord was behind him, behind them was the Demonic Sword Squad said to be the cult’s ultimate elite, and then the Demonic Warriors of the branch altar as well.

Had he ever walked while leading them?

Juyang felt as if he were dreaming. For no reason, he felt both proud and pleased.

Then he was suddenly frightened. Any one of them, if singled out, would be someone who would cause an uproar among ordinary martial artists.

When such frightening thoughts entered his mind, Juyang glanced at Geom Mugeuk, who was walking alongside him. Whether when he had first come to borrow money, or now that he had been revealed as the Young Cult Leader, he was exactly the same. He didn’t put on airs, nor did he act intimidating. How could a person be like that?

But even so, he did not dare speak to him.

He missed the days when they could chat comfortably. He missed the days when Geom Mugeuk had been a debtor.

While having such silly thoughts, before he knew it, they had arrived at Jeokpae’s manor.

Juyang lowered his voice and spoke in a whisper.

“I will have them open the door.”

Before those words had even finished.

Bang!

A Demonic Sword Squad warrior stepped forward and smashed the door, and the warriors poured inside.

Bladesmen popped out from all directions, then flinched in surprise. The Demonic Sword Squad warriors unleashed demonic qi, revealing who they were.

“If you don’t wish to die, all of you, leave.”

As the bladesmen looked at one another and hesitated.

The Demonic Sword Squad warrior who had warned them swung his sword. A streak of sword qi passed between the bladesmen and cut through the stone pagoda standing in front of the rear garden.

That single stone pagoda saved them all. As they fled rumbling toward the entrance, the warriors kicked their backsides and chased them all out. They ran, clutching their heads.

The Demonic Sword Squad warriors did not hesitate. When they smashed the second door and entered, hidden weapons poured down from the rooftops all around.

The warriors in the lead lightly swung their swords and knocked away the hidden weapons.

“From now on, throw even one more hidden weapon, and you die.”

Those who had thrown hidden weapons from the rooftops froze. Only then did they confirm that their opponents were Demonic Warriors.

“You fools, you ought to see who you’re throwing at.”

Geom Mugeuk passed by with a word, and the warriors followed after him. Those who had thrown the hidden weapons fled in the opposite direction.

Juyang, who had been watching, was bewildered by this unfamiliar sight.

He had known well that the Demonic Warriors were fearsome people, but to this extent? How terrifying it had been every time he passed this place. Jeokpae’s fortress was a walking path for the Demonic Warriors.

Beyond the next door, over ten martial artists blocked the way.

They were the ones Jeokpae had so boastfully claimed were masters of combined assault techniques. Juyang remembered Jeokpae saying something that was either a sigh or a boast—that they cost hundreds of taels a month. Jeokpae was a man who had spared no expense to protect his own life with money squeezed from blood.

As they formed a sword formation to confront them, the Demonic Sword Squad surged forward.

“Shall I show you what a real combined assault is?”

The Demonic Sword Squad did not even need to form a sword formation to intimidate them. The clustered martial artists scattered in all directions. Watching that sight, Juyang shook his head.

‘What exactly were those hundreds of taels a month spent on?’

Of course, he understood. What kind of fear it must be when the Demonic Sword Squad pushes in as intruders.

They seemed to push forward haphazardly to instill fear, but the Demonic Warriors had order. They seemed to simply walk, yet they thoroughly surveyed their surroundings. When one person moved, another filled that spot, maintaining formation. Their formations and responses differed depending on the terrain and the opponent. It was a skilled movement born of long training.

The three men at the final gate were experts who looked as though they must have cost not hundreds, but thousands of taels.

“We are the Jiangxi Three Ghosts. Who dares!”

In that moment, the eldest of the Three Ghosts recognized Branch Altar Lord Ho Gyeong.

“Why are you…?”

Then Ho Gyeong made a shushing gesture at him, and shook his head with an expression that said it was not their time to step in now. That alone was sufficient warning.

“Thank you for letting us pass.”

It was a situation where even if there had been a hundred ghosts rather than three, they could not have opposed them. Grateful to be spared, the Three Ghosts also left that place.

Thus they arrived deep inside the inner courtyard without spilling a single drop of blood.

The final door was open, and Jeokpae was waiting in the courtyard.

He bowed at the waist with as composed a face as possible.

“I greet you, distinguished guests.”

The reason Jeokpae had not fled was that he had misunderstood the current situation.

He thought that after punishing those who impersonated Demonic Warriors, the real Demonic Warriors had come. Since Juyang had come with them, it was only natural he would think so.

“If you had sent word beforehand, such mistakes would not have happened. My subordinates have committed a grave discourtesy. I apologize.”

Jeokpae carefully observed the visitors. He confirmed that Branch Altar Lord Ho Gyeong had come. He also confirmed that the captain of the Demonic Sword Squad was nowhere to be seen.

When he had entrusted the handling of this matter to Jungak previously, it had seemed likely he would make use of the Branch Altar Lord. Indeed, it appeared he had used the Branch Altar Lord for today’s affair.

‘And he pockets all the money alone!’

Jeokpae found the current situation a bit strange. The Ho Gyeong he knew was not one to take bribes. Why had this man come here?

Jeokpae glanced at Juyang. He had sent him to die along with them in his heart.

‘What a tenacious lifeline. Yes, you just work until you die.’

Jeokpae asked with his eyes—just what was this situation now? What could Juyang say? He simply turned his head, avoiding his gaze.

Then Geom Mugeuk held out a coin to Jeokpae.

“Here, take it.”

“What is this?”

“It’s interest.”

In that instant, Jeokpae’s heart plummeted. Two coins placed atop the corpses of his subordinates came to mind, and an ominous premonition swept over him.

“I told you to take it.”

The moment Jeokpae unwittingly received it.

Whoosh.

Bang!

His hand was slammed into the ground by the coin’s weight.

“Aaaaack!”

He shrieked, but no one came to save him.

“Now, one more left. Take this too.”

Jeokpae looked up at Geom Mugeuk with an expression of agony.

“W-why are you doing this?”

“Why? You borrowed money, so you’re paying interest.”

“You don’t have to.”

“Don’t you have Abanggung too? You have to collect interest to maintain it, don’t you?”

“Please forgive me! Please spare me!”

But it was not something he could refuse simply because he did not want to take it. Due to an invisible force, his other hand spread open.

Whoosh!

Bang!

In the end, his other hand was crushed as well.

His prostrated appearance, palms facing upward, looked almost like he was bowing to the heavens. Having lived his entire life without knowing pain, he made a terrible fuss about it hurting.

Juyang understood.

That Geom Mugeuk did not kill Jeokpae right away, but made him receive the coins like this, was to show him. That the individual stories contained in the interest he would collect from now on were this heavy.

Juyang lowered his head with an expression showing that he understood.

Then Geom Mugeuk lightly struck out with one palm.

Smack!

Jeokpae’s head burst, and he died instantly. It was the moment his life ended, having led countless people’s lives to ruin while living solely for money. Though he had earned so much money, it was also a lonely death where nothing remained.

Geom Mugeuk had all the subordinates from Tongjeonso called over.

After lining them all up, he introduced Juyang.

“From now on, the master of Tongjeonso is Juyang here. Those who are dissatisfied, leave right now. You will be allowed to leave alive.”

A few among those gathered who did not get along with Juyang left that place. Those who had wanted to quit this work but thought this was their opportunity also left together. Thus those who would leave, left.

Geom Mugeuk sternly warned those who remained.

“From now on, follow the new leader’s words well.”

“Yes!”

And so the master of Tongjeonso changed overnight.

Ho Gyeong inwardly admired Geom Mugeuk’s unstoppable stride. These were not things possible merely because one’s martial arts were high. It was a stride that only someone who clearly knew what he was doing and what he had to do from now on could take.

“Now, let us all drink together today.”

It was a pleasant and good day for Ho Gyeong too. But he could not hide his worry.

“Today, the core of the Jiangxi Branch is all gathered here. Shouldn’t we send back even some of the troops?”

“Are you worried that the Four Ways Alliance might pull something?”

“We sent separate word, so that shouldn’t be the case… but still, this is the first time something like this has happened.”

Having lived concerned about the Four Ways Alliance every day since his appointment, it was only natural that he would be tense and sensitive in this situation.

“You don’t need to worry. The Four Ways Alliance isn’t the old Four Ways Alliance anymore.”

Geom Mugeuk’s confidence came from trusting Bisain. Upon hearing this news, he might rush to a tavern with his subordinates, but he was not someone who would open the armory.

Thus a large drinking party was prepared at the training ground.

They brought all the good liquor Jeokpae had collected, and had all kinds of dishes bought from the famous inns and taverns of that area.

They had almost never gathered like this to drink together. Especially for the Demonic Warriors of the branch altar and the Demonic Sword Squad to mingle together was truly a first.

When everyone’s cups were filled, Geom Mugeuk raised his cup high in a loud voice.

“Now, today let us forget all worldly affairs and drink to our hearts’ content!”

Everyone drank refreshingly.

The gazes of the Demonic Sword Squad warriors toward Geom Mugeuk were exceedingly favorable. Both the comfort he had given them earlier, and his martial arts prowess that overwhelmed everyone.

Geom Mugeuk mingled with them comfortably.

He talked about the main altar and about the Demon Lords. Especially when he spoke of the Demon Army, which the Demonic Sword Squad considered a rival, everyone’s eyes sparkled.

And that wasn’t all. From the Central Plains to the lands beyond the frontiers, with his broad experience and his unique gift of gab, the Demonic Warriors lost track of time, immersed in Geom Mugeuk’s stories.

Even amidst that, Juyang sat motionless beside Geom Mugeuk. Even when he needed to urinate, he did not go. What if someone else sat there while he was away, and he had to sit among the Demonic Warriors? He’d rather fight them all! He desperately defended the seat next to Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk, who had been chatting and playing with the warriors for a long while, came over to Ho Gyeong, who had been drinking quietly alone. As he left behind Juyang, who had tried to follow, Juyang made an expression like he had been abandoned alone in hell.

“It’s tiring playing with the kids.”

At those words, Ho Gyeong smiled, and Geom Mugeuk also smiled and added.

“I’m the youngest by age, yet I say such things, don’t I?”

“Just hearing you talk, you sounded like the eldest of them all. When in the world did you have such experiences?”

“Words picked up here and there.”

The two emptied their cups. Ho Gyeong poured alcohol for Geom Mugeuk and asked.

“But how do you remember my past affairs?”

“The moment I saw the Branch Altar Lord, I remembered that day at the banquet hall. It was an impressive remark, and your face is exactly the same as it was then.”

“What remark. I have aged greatly.”

This time, Geom Mugeuk filled his cup.

“It must be hard trying to take care of everything so meticulously?”

“It’s my personality, what can I do? If I leave it be, I cannot sleep.”

There must be the sorrow felt by a man of principle like him. The hardship unique to the one who is criticized by others despite doing everything for the best. And how could he not wish to live comfortably?

Geom Mugeuk said, emptying his cup.

“If you do difficult things for a long time, doesn’t that hardship sometimes become strength?”

It was the feeling he had felt when gathering materials for the great technique before his regression.

At first, it had been so hard and lonely. Back then, he had fallen asleep with this expectation—that tomorrow would be better.

As time passed, he had thought like this. Tomorrow will be hard too. It is harder because I try to avoid hardship. Tomorrow will be hard, and the day after will be hard too. It is not just me; everyone is struggling.

When even more years had passed, he realized. That in the end, what had made him grow the most was that hardship. That it was both a tiresome enemy and his only friend.

“That is why the Branch Altar Lord looks strong.”

Ho Gyeong looked at Geom Mugeuk’s deep eyes, which did not match his age, and felt that he too had been through many trials. Yes, he must have gone through all sorts of things fighting for succession until he became the Young Cult Leader.

“Cheer up, Young Cult Leader!”

“Let us both be strong!”

And so the two talked about the affairs of the Jiangxi Branch until late. Geom Mugeuk silently listened to everything Ho Gyeong said.

* * *

He found Juyang in the backyard.

Having been avoiding the Demonic Warriors, he was sitting alone in front of a bonfire.

“What are you doing?”

“Ah, I was just here.”

Geom Mugeuk sat down beside him.

“Everyone drinks well.”

“That’s because you haven’t seen Drunken Horse.”

Even Chwima, and those demonic cultists who drank without ever tiring, were people from another world.

But he felt he would miss the man before him. Though he was the one furthest away, even within that other world.

“Will I be able to see you again, Young Cult Leader?”

“I don’t know either. We might see each other often, or we might never meet again until the day we die.”

“I’ll be working diligently, so if you come to Jiangxi, please be sure to stop by. I’ll send reports related to the work without fail.”

“Juyang.”

“Yes.”

Perhaps because of the firelight, or because he was nervous, Juyang’s face was flushed red.

“One day, a crisis will come for you. That crisis will tempt you with these words: ‘Ah, what the hell, you only live once anyway, don’t you?’ Juyang, the sins you’ve already committed are great, so if you do something foolish, I won’t be able to forgive you. So if that temptation comes to you, come find me.”

“You mean I should come to the Demonic Cult’s main headquarters?”

“Yes. Come, stand at the main gate, and ask for me.”

Juyang imagined the scene for a moment. Himself standing at the main gate of the Demonic Cult’s headquarters, saying, “I have come to see the Young Cult Leader.” Then the demonic cultists at the gate would ask, “Who are you?” No, that wasn’t right. Since he was a guest of the Young Cult Leader, it would be, “And who might you be?” Then he would say, “I’m a loan shark from faraway Jiangxi.” Ah, he did not particularly want to imagine what came next.

“I’ll work desperately hard and live so I never have to come find you.”

At Juyang’s joke, Geom Mugeuk laughed with him. A Young Cult Leader who would laugh with him like this.

“Now, this is my final gift.”

Geom Mugeuk handed him two nyang.

Juyang respectfully accepted the money. They were the two coins he had kept receiving ever since he met Geom Mugeuk. So now he was receiving them for the last time.

Clutching the coins tightly, Juyang asked,

“Why are you being so good to someone like me, who is nothing more than a mere loan shark?”

“I hear that question often.”

Geom Mugeuk spoke while gazing into the campfire.

“Perhaps I’m simply that kind of person.”

Juyang sprang to his feet and performed a deep bow to Geom Mugeuk.

“I will never forget the grace of being given a new life. I don’t know whether I’m a pine caterpillar, a maggot, a larva, or whatever else, but whatever I am, I’ll make sure to spread my wings at least once.”

Without a word, Geom Mugeuk merely watched the sparks leap from the campfire and dance upward through the air.

* * *

Two days later, Geom Mugeuk bid farewell to Hogyeong and the other demonic cultists of the Jiangxi Branch.

“Are you returning to the main headquarters?”

“If I stay any longer, I’ll only get in the way of your work.”

When Hogyeong said nothing, Geom Mugeuk spoke playfully.

“You really must think so.”

“You are not someone I can order around, after all.”

The two looked at each other and laughed.

“The main headquarters will appoint a new Demon Sword Unit Leader before long.”

“I suppose I’ll have to fight another difficult battle.”

A day will come when hardship becomes strength.

Geom Mugeuk conveyed the words he had spoken that day with his gaze.

As long as Hogyeong was here, there would be no need to worry about the Jiangxi Branch.

Then, the inspection of the Jiangxi Branch was complete.

* * *

After parting with the Jiangxi Branch, the place Geom Mugeuk visited was the home of the coachman, Pyeonjung.

“I’m looking for a skilled coachman.”

Pyeonjung had just received news from the Tongjeonso that he no longer had to repay his debt. They said that, after calculating an appropriate interest rate, they would return the money he had paid in excess of the principal.

At the unbelievable news, he had clung to his wife and sobbed. Not knowing what was happening, the children had cried along with them.

With his eyes swollen, Pyeonjung could tell. He did not know exactly what had happened, but the man before him was the one who had accomplished it.

Pyeonjung lowered his head and conveyed his deep gratitude in his heart.

“Where shall I take you?”

Then came the most perilous destination in Pyeonjung’s entire history as a coachman.

“The headquarters of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

When he had first been told to go to the Jiangxi Branch of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, Pyeonjung’s heart had dropped.

But now, he was not afraid. Instead, he smiled brightly and repaid him with the thing he did best.

“I will take you safely to your destination.”
